### [1] [An action of the affine Yangian $Y_{t_1,t_2}(\widehat{\mathfrak{gl}}_1)$ on the cohomology of the moduli space of stable sheaves on surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27473)
*Claudio Pfammatter*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该论文研究仿射Yangian Y_{t1,t2}(ĝl_1)在光滑射影曲面S上稳定层模空间M的奇异上同调上的作用。通过嵌套模空间的相交理论分析，证明了该Yangian表示的存在，避免了传统上所需的K理论工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上建立仿射Yangian在模空间上同调上的作用依赖于K理论的复杂机制。作者希望绕开K理论方法，通过更直接的相交理论途径来建立这一作用，从而将经典构造推广到完整的Yangian结构。

Method: 通过对嵌套模空间（nested moduli spaces）的相交理论（intersection-theoretic）分析来构造仿射Yangian的作用。该方法将经典构造扩展到完整的Yangian框架，利用相交理论工具而非K理论机制。

Result: 成功证明了仿射Yangian Y_{t1,t2}(ĝl_1)在稳定层模空间M的奇异上同调上的表示，且该证明不依赖传统所需的K理论机制。

Conclusion: 交理论方法为建立仿射Yangian在模空间上同调上的作用提供了一条独立于K理论的替代途径，扩展了经典构造的适用范围。

Abstract: We investigate the action of the affine Yangian $Y_{t_1,t_2}(\widehat{\mathfrak{gl}}_1)$ on the singular cohomology of the moduli space of stable sheaves $\mathcal{M}$ on a smooth projective surface $S$. Through an intersection-theoretic analysis of nested moduli spaces, we obtain a proof of the representation $$Y_{t_1,t_2}(\widehat{\mathfrak{gl}}_1) \curvearrowright H_{\mathcal{M}}.$$ This intersection-theoretic approach extends classical constructions to the full Yangian structure and circumvents the $K$-theoretic machinery traditionally required to establish this action.

</details>


### [2] [Orbifold Degenerations of Hirzebruch Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27652)
*Juan Pablo Zúñiga*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了 Hirzebruch 曲面的轨道退化情形。主要定理表明，每一个这样的退化 X 都可以作为某个环面曲面（toric surface）的部分光滑化得到。作者给出了当 -K_X 不是 nef 时奇异性的组合描述，并针对 k≥3 的 Hirzebruch 曲面 \mathbb{F}_k 给出了所有可能中心纤维的显式描述；对 k≤1 的情况，利用三维极小模型纲领将问题约化为反典范除子为 nef 的中心纤维情形，并最终通过研究 Fano 多边形的突变（mutation）产生的双有理几何，对带 T-奇点的度 8 环面 del Pezzo 曲面进行了分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在完整刻画 Hirzebruch 曲面轨道退化的中心纤维，补充此前与 G. Urzúa 合作工作中关于 -K_X 为 ample 情形的结果，将研究拓展到 -K_X 非 nef 的互补情形，从而系统地理解 Hirzebruch 曲面退化中的奇点结构。

Method: 本文采用多重方法：首先利用环面几何建立主要定理（轨道退化作为环面曲面的部分光滑化）；随后结合组合方法描述奇点（尤其是 -K_X 非 nef 时的情形）；对 k≥3 的情况给出中心纤维的显式分类；对 k≤1 的情况，运用三维极小模型纲领（MMP）进行约化，将问题转化为反典范除子 nef 的情形；最后通过 Fano 多边形的突变研究带 T-奇点的 toric del Pezzo 曲面的双有理几何并完成分类。

Result: 主要结果为：(1) 证明了任意 Hirzebruch 曲面的轨道退化都能表示为某个环面曲面的部分光滑化；(2) 给出了 -K_X 非 nef 时奇异性的完整组合描述；(3) 对 \mathbb{F}_k (k≥3) 给出了所有可能中心纤维的显式描述；(4) 对 k≤1 的情形通过 MMP 将问题约化后，将剩余情形（即带 T-奇点的度 8 环面 del Pezzo 曲面）通过 Fano 多边形突变的双有理几何完整分类。

Conclusion: 该工作完成了 Hirzebruch 曲面轨道退化研究的互补情形，即 -K_X 非 nef 的情况，与先前 ample 情形的结果共同构成了完整的理论框架。结果表明此类退化具有高度的环面几何结构性，且中心纤维的分类可以通过组合工具（Fano 多边形突变）和极小模型纲领系统地完成。

Abstract: We study orbifold degenerations of Hirzebruch surfaces. Our main theorem shows that every such degeneration $X$ arises as a partial smoothing of a toric surface. We then give a combinatorial description of the singularities that arise when $-K_X$ is not nef. This complements previous joint work with G. Urzúa, which treated the case in which $-K_X$ is ample. For Hirzebruch surfaces $\mathbb{F}_k$ with $k\geq 3$, we obtain an explicit description of all possible central fibers. For $k\leq 1$, we use the threefold minimal model program to reduce the problem to the case of central fibers whose anticanonical divisor is nef. The remaining surfaces are toric del Pezzo surfaces of degree $8$ with T-singularities. We classify these by studying the birational geometry arising from mutations of Fano polygons.

</details>


### [3] [Finding large families of rational curves through Bend-and-Break](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27696)
*Eric Jovinelly,Brian Lehmann,Eric Riedl*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新构造，可从高阶曲线族中分离出大次有理曲线，深化了有理曲线与反典范除子正性之间的联系。研究表明具有大Fujita不变量的簇具有大族有理曲线，并在某些奇异Fano簇上构造了自由有理曲线。作为直接推论，证明了一般Fano超曲面（指数≥3）上足够大次数的g亏格曲线空间具有预期维数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索有理曲线与反典范除子正性（Fano簇性质）之间的深层联系，特别是通过Fujita不变量来刻画簇上存在大族有理曲线的条件，并推广到高阶曲线族及奇异Fano簇的情形。

Method: 提出一种新的构造方法，能够从高亏格曲线族中"断裂"（break off）出大次数的有理曲线。该方法将有理曲线的存在性与Fujita不变量的下界联系起来，并推广到奇异Fano簇上自由有理曲线的构造。

Result: 证明了具有大Fujita不变量的簇必然包含大族有理曲线；在奇异Fano簇上构造了自由有理曲线；并由此证明了一般Fano超曲面（指数≥3）上，足够大次数的亏格g曲线模空间具有预期维数。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了Fujita不变量与有理曲线族之间存在性的定量联系，深化了几何有理性与Fano簇正性理论的关系，并给出了Fano超曲面上曲线模空间维数的精确刻画。

Abstract: We present a new construction that allows us to break off large-degree rational curves from families of higher genus curves. Our construction and results deepen the connection between rational curves and positivity of the anticanonical divisor. Specifically, we show that varieties with large Fujita invariant admit large families of rational curves. We also construct free rational curves on certain singular Fano varieties. As an explicit consequence of our results, we prove that for a general Fano hypersurface of index at least 3, all spaces of genus g curves of sufficiently large degree have the expected dimension.

</details>


### [4] [Singularity Categories of Simple Singularities in Positive Characteristic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27738)
*Yuta Takashima*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了正特征代数闭域上同维简单奇点的奇异范畴，证明这些范畴在三角范畴意义下不等价（除非解析同构），与特征零情形一致；但与特征零不同，正特征下无法仅凭Auslander-Reiten箭图区分简单奇点。为此，将Hua-Keller定理推广到正特征，即孤立超曲面奇点的dg奇异范畴的第0次Hochschild上同调同构于定义多项式的Tyurina代数。作为应用，确定了二维有理双重点（即二维简单奇点）的奇异范畴为标准范畴的充要条件是定义多项式为加权齐次的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特征零情况下，简单奇点的奇异范畴被证明具有区分性且可通过Auslander-Reiten箭图辨别，而正特征情形下这些结论是否成立并不清楚。本文旨在探究正特征下简单奇点的奇异范畴的分类性质，并解决AR箭图不足以区分奇点的问题。

Method: 将Hua和Keller关于特征零的定理推广到正特征，该定理断言孤立超曲面奇点的dg奇异范畴的第0次Hochschild上同调同构于定义多项式的Tyurina代数；利用这一工具对正特征下简单奇点的奇异范畴进行分类判别。

Result: 证明了正特征下同维简单奇点的奇异范畴不等价（除非解析同构），与特征零一致；同时指出正特征下AR箭图无法区分这些奇点。作为应用，证明了二维有理双重点的奇异范畴是标准范畴当且仅当定义多项式是加权齐次的。

Conclusion: 正特征下简单奇点的奇异范畴保持了与特征零类似的分类性质（不等价性），但需要借助Hochschild上同调等更精细的工具来实现区分（因为AR箭图失效）。标准性判据给出了加权齐次条件这一简洁刻画。

Abstract: We study the singularity categories of simple singularities of the same dimension over an algebraically closed field of positive characteristic, and show that, as in characteristic zero, these categories are not equivalent as triangulated categories unless the underlying singularities are analytically isomorphic. In contrast to the characteristic zero case, simple singularities in positive characteristic cannot be distinguished solely from the Auslander-Reiten quivers of their singularity categories. To address this, we extend to positive characteristics a theorem by Hua and Keller, which asserts that the 0th Hochschild cohomology of the dg singularity category of an isolated hypersurface singularity in characteristic zero is isomorphic to the Tyurina algebra of the defining polynomial. Furthermore as an application, we determine the condition for the singularity category of a rational double point (i.e., a simple singularity of dimension two) to be standard. We prove that such a category is standard if and only if the defining polynomial is weighted homogeneous.

</details>


### [5] [$K_2$-regularity is equivalent to smoothness for toric varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27761)
*Christian Haesemeyer,George Henderson-Walshe*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了仿射环面簇（affine toric varieties）在特征零下，如果其$K_2$-正则（即满足某些$K_2$代数条件），则必定是光滑的，将Wanchun Shen关于仿射局部完全交的相应结果推广到环面簇情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wanchun Shen近期证明了特征零下任意维数的$K_2$-正则仿射局部完全交都是光滑的。本文的目标是将这一结论推广到仿射环面簇上，进一步丰富$K_2$-正则性与光滑性之间的关联研究。

Method: 利用仿射环面簇的典型代数结构（对应有理锥的半群代数），结合$K_2$-正则性质以及特征零条件下的代数几何工具进行分析和证明。

Result: 证明了特征零下，任何$K_2$-正则的仿射环面簇都是光滑的。

Conclusion: $K_2$-正则性蕴含光滑性这一结论不仅对仿射局部完全交成立，对仿射环面簇同样成立，进一步验证了$K_2$-正则性作为光滑性刻画条件的普适性。

Abstract: Recent work by Wanchun Shen shows that $K_2$-regular affine local complete intersections of any dimension in characteristic zero are smooth. In this note, we show that the same holds for affine toric varieties.

</details>


### [6] [The Segre invariant and Brill-Noether theory on Hirzebruch surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27769)
*Mauricio Rivera-Vega*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Hirzebruch曲面Σ_n上具有固定Chern类的秩二H-稳定向量丛的Segre不变量，确定了每个向量丛可能的Segre不变量取值，计算了这些丛的全局截面数量，并给出了在Brill-Noether理论中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Segre不变量是研究秩二向量丛几何结构的重要工具，而Hirzebruch曲面作为最简单的有理曲面之一，其上稳定向量丛的Segre不变量与Brill-Noether理论的关系尚未被系统研究。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 通过系统分析秩二H-稳定向量丛在Hirzebruch曲面上的结构，利用Chern类约束与稳定性的条件，确定Segre不变量的全部可能取值；进而通过计算全局截面的维数，将结果应用于Brill-Noether理论的推广研究。

Result: 对Hirzebruch曲面Σ_n上具有固定Chern类的秩二H-稳定向量丛，完整确定了其Segre不变量的可能取值，并给出了相应的全局截面数量公式，同时获得了在Brill-Noether理论中的新应用结果。

Conclusion: 本文为Hirzebruch曲面上稳定向量丛的Segre不变量和Brill-Noether理论提供了系统的刻画，深化了对有理曲面上向量丛几何结构的理解。

Abstract: We study the Segre invariant on rank two $H$-stable vector bundles with fixed Chern classes on Hirzebruch surfaces $Σ_{n}$. For each vector bundle on these surfaces, we determine the possible values of the Segre invariant and compute the number of global sections of these bundles and present applications to Brill-Noether Theory.

</details>


### [7] [Metric-geometric Chow theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27810)
*José Edson Sampaio*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文推广了o-极小Chow定理，给出若干等价的几何与度量刻画，证明纯d维整复解析集X⊂ℂⁿ为代数集当且仅当满足一系列关于无穷远切锥的Hausdorff测度为零或严格小于ℂPᵈ测度的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Peterzil和Starchenko在2009年证明了o-极小Chow定理（o-极小可定义的整复解析集必为代数集），本文旨在将这一深刻的几何结果推广到更一般的度量与几何框架中，不依赖于o-极小/可定义性条件，而仅通过无穷远切锥的Hausdorff测度来刻画代数性。

Method: 作者引入实切锥C(X,∞)与复切锥C_C(X,∞)等概念，并利用其Hausdorff测度作为几何度量来刻画代数性。给出了五个等价的判定条件：(1) X为复代数集；(2) 实切锥的(2d+1)维Hausdorff测度为零；(3) 复切锥的(2d+2)维Hausdorff测度为零；(4) 复切锥在射影空间中的像Z(X,∞)的2d维Hausdorff测度为零；(5) 对任意适当的投影π，Z(Y,∞)的2d维Hausdorff测度严格小于ℂPᵈ的2d维Hausdorff测度。

Result: 作者证明了上述五个条件相互等价，从而实现了对o-极小Chow定理的度量及几何版本的推广。这些新判定条件仅依赖于无穷远切锥的Hausdorff测度，较o-极小可定义性条件更加自然且适用范围更广。

Conclusion: 本文通过无穷远切锥的Hausdorff测度给出了代数性的多种等价刻画，从几何与度量角度推广了o-极小Chow定理，为判定整复解析集是否为代数集提供了新的有效工具。

Abstract: In 2009, Peterzil and Starchenko proved the following beautiful generalization of Chow's theorem: An entire complex analytic set $X\subset \mathbb{C}^n$ that is definable in an o-minimal structure on $\mathbb{R}$ must be an algebraic set. This result is known nowadays as the o-minimal Chow theorem. In this article, we present some geometric and metric versions of Chow's theorem that generalize the o-minimal Chow's theorem. For instance, we prove that if $X\subset \mathbb{C}^n$ is a pure $d$-dimensional entire complex analytic set, then the following statements are equivalent:
  (1) $X$ is a complex algebraic set;
  (2) $\mathcal{H}^{2d+1}(C(X,\infty))=0$, where $\mathcal{H}^{k}(A)$ denotes the $k$-dimensional Hausdorff measure of $A$, and $C(X,\infty)$ denotes the tangent cone at infinity of $X$;
  (3) $\mathcal{H}^{2d+2}(C_{\mathbb{C}}(X,\infty))=0$, where $C_{\mathbb{C}}(X,\infty)$ denotes the complex tangent cone at infinity of $X$;
  (4) $\mathcal{H}^{2d}(Z(X,\infty))=0$, where for $A\subset \mathbb{C}^k$, $Z(A,\infty)=\{[v]\in \mathbb{C}P^{k-1};v\in C_{\mathbb{C}}(A,\infty)\}$;
  (5) For any $k\in\{d+1,...,n\}$ and for any projection $π\colon \mathbb{C}^{n}\to \mathbb{C}^{k}$ such that $π^{-1}(0)\cap C_{\mathbb{C}}(X,\infty)=\{0\}$ and $Y=π(X)$, $\mathcal{H}^{2d}(Z(Y,\infty))<\mathcal{H}^{2d}(\mathbb{C}P^{d})$.

</details>


### [8] [Independence of the Grothendieck classes of twisted symplectic flag varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28181)
*Goncalo Tabuada*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文利用非交换动机理论证明了扭曲辛旗簇的Grothendieck类在代数簇的Grothendieck环中是线性无关的，且进一步是齐次二次无关的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究代数簇的Grothendieck环中特定簇类的代数独立性是一个重要且困难的问题。扭曲辛旗簇作为一类重要的齐性空间，其Grothendieck类的独立性此前尚未被证明，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 本文采用非交换动机（noncommutative motives）的理论框架作为研究工具，通过将代数簇的Grothendieck环中的问题转化为非交换动机范畴中的问题来处理，从而证明扭曲辛旗簇的Grothendieck类满足线性独立性和齐次二次独立性。

Result: 成功证明了扭曲辛旗簇的Grothendieck类在Grothendieck环中既是线性独立的，又是齐次二次独立的（homogeneous-quadratic independent）。

Conclusion: 非交换动机理论为研究Grothendieck环中代数簇类的代数独立性提供了一种有效的工具，本文的结果丰富了关于扭曲旗簇Grothendieck类性质的认识。

Abstract: In this short note, making use of the theory of noncommutative motives, we prove that the Grothendieck classes of the twisted symplectic flag varieties are linearly independent and moreover homogeneous-quadratic independent in the Grothendieck ring of algebraic varieties.

</details>


### [9] [On Frobenius rigidity for motivic cohomology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27834)
*Thomas H. Geisser*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该论文研究光滑紧致簇的motivic与étale motivic上同调在代数闭域基域扩张下的行为，证明有限系数下与基域无关（除外特征处），并结合完美幂幺群概形结构理论分析Frobenius纤维（Weil-étale上同调）的基域无关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解上同调理论在基域扩张下的不变性是代数几何与算术几何中的基本问题。对于定义在不同代数闭域上的同类簇，其motivic和étale上同调是否一致直接关系到这些不变量是否具备"几何本质"而非依赖基域的解析选择。特别是在特征p情形，需要厘清étale上同调与motivic上同调对基域的依赖差异。

Method: 论文采用双管齐下的方法：一方面系统比较有限系数下motivic与étale motivic上同调在基域扩张下的行为，特别区分非特征与特征两种情况；另一方面对有限域上定义的簇，借助完美幂幺群概形（perfect unipotent group schemes）的结构理论分析Frobenius映射的纤维，即Weil-étale上同调，证明其基域无关性。同时论文还探讨了整数系数情形下的结果与反例。

Result: 主要结果显示：(1) 有限系数下motivic与étale motivic上同调在代数闭域扩张下除特征外均不变；(2) 在特征处，étale上同调依赖基域，而motivic上同调仅在权重0、1、dim X已知；(3) 对有限域上定义的簇，Weil-étale上同调（Frobenius纤维）在有限系数下与基域无关；(4) 提供整数系数下的若干结果与反例。

A major challenge in these setups is the limited detection efficiency of current detectors as to observe nonlocality or steering in the standard scenarios, one requires detectors above a certain critical efficiency which can only be achieved with superconducting detectors. Considering the simplest quantum network, we demonstrate here that quantum steering between two parties, can be demonstrated for any non-zero detection efficiency, if the sources in the network generate states above a critical visibility well-within the current practical limits. This form of quantum steering in networks, is termed swap-steering. Moreover, when the sources are perfect, swap-steering can be observed using any entangled measurement on the untrusted side, even with arbitrarily low detection efficiency. Consequently, two major loopholes, the detection-loophole as well as free-will loophole can be closed easily in quantum steering experiments, when implemented using networks.

Abstract: We present a flip-chip-based sensing scheme to measure effective microwave losses associated with target materials for quantum technologies, without requiring any device fabrication on the material under test. We further study the superconductor platinum silicon germanide (PtSiGe) prepared by thermal reaction with a deposited Pt film, finding high microwave losses that limit the suitability of the films studied here as the sole superconductor for high-quality resonator applications. Abstract: Ordering objectives for commuting phase terms can have many equal optima, yet prior methods do not characterize or exploit those ties. Among 24 sampled minimum-support-cost orders at 36 terms, direct-depth selection reduced opposite-SABRE-seed depth by 12.83% in all 20 aggregates, whereas a matched 24-restart control changed depth by only -0.41% (unresolved). Candidate rankings persisted across SABRE routing seeds, explaining why selection survived routing re-randomization. The depth benefit transferred to a second generator and to 48 terms, but reversed under BasicSwap. On a prospective IBM Heron panel, raw generator error shifted by -0.0025 (-0.59%); fixed-panel shot uncertainty excluded zero, but term-seed inference remained unresolved. Abstract: Quantum Fourier transforms (QFTs) are essential primitives in quantum algorithms. Using the Mackey-theoretic approach, we obtain explicit quantum circuits for the QFT over $\mathrm{GL}_2(F_q)$ that scale polynomially in $\log q$, rather than polynomially in $q$. Using the Clifford-theoretic approach, we obtain QFT circuits for wreath products $F\wr S_n$, whose cost depends on the cost of a QFT over $F$ and the size of its representation registers. This removes the restriction $|F|=\operatorname{poly}(n)$ required by previous generic constructions and can yield exponential improvements when $F$ itself has an efficient QFT. Together, these methods provide new systematic tools to construct QFTs for broad classes of finite groups.

Abstract: Recent examples have confirmed the common belief that quantum chaos is always suppressed in the presence of an environment. Here we show that this is not always the case. Abstract: Markovian open-system dynamics have widespread applications throughout quantum information science, including algorithmic state preparation. Their convergence is commonly quantified using the worst case global trace distance between the evolving and stationary states. However, this criterion can be unnecessarily stringent when only physically relevant observables are of interest. Here we introduce and study observable-specific mixing times. We prove that, for quasi-local, rapidly mixing Lindbladians, sums of geometrically local observables equilibrate in a time independent of system size, in contrast to the logarithmic dependence of global state mixing. This separation reduces the runtime of dissipative quantum algorithms, including quantum Gibbs samplers, for estimating quantities such as the Gibbs state energy and local order parameters, yielding an overall scaling that is linear in system size. Complementing this quantum result, we develop a quantum-inspired classical algorithm for estimating the same quantities. Its runtime is likewise linear in system size, but scaling exponentially in $\mathcal{O}\big(\log(1/ε)^D\big)$, where $D$ denotes the spatial dimension of the lattice. We further analyse non-interacting Lindbladians over qudits, fermions, and bosons, demonstrating that locality of observables is not always necessary for a qualitatively faster mixing. Small-scale simulations of quantum Gibbs samplers reveal no large hidden constants in our asymptotic analysis and show that the theoretical predictions closely capture the finite-size dynamics.

Abstract: Real-time quantum error correction requires classical decoders to process growing syndrome workloads with low and predictable latency. For quantum low-density parity-check (qLDPC) codes, iterative belief propagation (BP) repeatedly updates messages over sparse Tanner graphs, creating substantial memory-access and data-movement demands. Using uPIMulator and a data-qubit Pauli error model with ideal syndrome measurements, we compare throughput, per-syndrome processing time, logical error rate (LER), and single-syndrome tail latency against a 16-logical-CPU baseline. At a component-wise physical error probability of p=0.001 and one BP iteration, the projected aggregate kernel throughput of 2,560 DPUs reaches 1.071 x 10^7 decodes/s, compared with 1.22 x 10^6 decodes/s for the CPU, an 8.8x improvement. Abstract: Quantum state and process tomography constitute essential diagnostic tools in quantum information science, yet their standard formulations suffer from prohibitive computational scaling as the number of qubits grows. However, the configuration provides the largest global-local ergotropy gap, which varies with the exchange interaction, initial state and thermal coupling. Suppression of the gap at large $J$ is typically observed in the case of stronger thermalisation, although enhancement of intermediate-coupling may occur. These results establish that total extractable work, local coherence and collective work advantage quantify the distinct aspects of quantum battery performances. The initial distribution of coherence and the spatial arrangement thus provide complementary mechanisms for regulating locally and collectively extractable work in interacting open quantum batteries.

Abstract: Quantum federated learning enables collaborative model training across quantum devices without sharing raw data, and it faces the data and hardware heterogeneity inherent to noisy quantum devices. Abstract: We prove recovery theorems for $α$-$z$ Rényi divergences under normal unital positive maps between von Neumann algebras. As a further application of our methods, we solve a problem discussed by Haagerup and Stormer: Every conditional expectation of a von Neumann algebra onto a JW*-subalgebra factors through a conditional expectation onto the generated von Neumann subalgebra.

Abstract: Quantum sensors are specified by a headline sensitivity, yet applications also demand accuracy and reliability. The dominant limiter of one metric is often known, but no method resolves how interacting mechanisms combine into a signed, per-mechanism budget for each metric. We introduce a framework that computes a sensor's sensitivity, accuracy, and robustness from one open-system simulation and attributes each to its limiting mechanism. For a nitrogen-vacancy diamond ensemble the attribution inverts across metrics: dephasing limits sensitivity, the thermal ground-state shift limits accuracy, and optical leakage limits robustness. At identical sensitivity the recovered-field bias spans $8$ to $1500$\,nT, so tuning to sensitivity alone can miss the accuracy target by two orders of magnitude. The same modeling transfers to a cesium optically pumped magnetometer recording a human magnetocardiogram. As a digital twin, it predicts the gain from addressing each limiter, so sensors can be designed to the required metrics.

</details>

Abstract: We experimentally study error mitigation and finite-shot sampling on superconducting quantum hardware under a constrained execution budget. The study combines calibration-aware qubit selection, circuit-depth scaling, zero-noise extrapolation, dynamical decoupling, readout-error mitigation, and repeated-shot estimation on an IBM Quantum processor. Experiments are organized across ideal simulation, noise-model simulation, and physical-device execution to separate sampling uncertainty from device-induced error. We investigate how mitigation performance changes with circuit depth, effective noise scale, qubit connectivity, and measurement budget, and quantify accuracy using expectation-value error, mean-squared error, statistical uncertainty, and mitigation gain. A fixed hardware-execution budget is used to evaluate shot allocation strategies and repeated measurements without relying on unlimited sampling. The resulting analysis provides a hardware-aware characterization of when mitigation improves expectation-value estimation and when finite-shot fluctuations offset the benefit of additional mitigation overhead. The implementation uses contemporary Qiskit and IBM Quantum Runtime workflows and is designed to provide reproducible experimental evidence for error-mitigation studies on current quantum processors.

</details>

Abstract: We introduce a neural message-passing framework for decoding general concatenated stabilizer codes. Soft beliefs propagate bidirectionally across concatenation levels, and lightweight neural networks learn only to aggregate incoming messages. For the concatenated $[[15,7,3]]$ quantum Hamming code, the resulting decoder achieves substantially higher thresholds than the state-of-the-art bidirectional hard-decision decoder under both bit-flip and depolarizing noise. In particular, the depolarizing pseudo-threshold nearly doubles, from $6.5\%$ to $12.3\%$. For many-hypercube codes, a decoder fine-tuned on circuit-level errors in Knill's teleportation-based error correction can achieve lower logical-CNOT failure rates than their dedicated decoder, using a fixed number of message-passing iterations instead of extensive combinatorial search. Our framework provides a generic decoding tool for exploring the design space of concatenated codes, including non-CSS constructions, toward low-overhead fault tolerance.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辛嵌入问题是辛几何的核心问题之一。已有的Hutchings判据仅适用于凸奇形域之间的嵌入，无法处理涉及凹奇形域或半弱凸奇形域的更广泛嵌入情形。作者旨在推广该判据，建立更一般的情形的嵌入障碍，并验证其在实际问题中的有效性。

Method: 利用嵌入接触同调（ECH），系统研究四维奇形域间的辛嵌入。将Hutchings的凸到凸嵌入判据分情形推广到：凸域到凹域、半弱凸域到凸域、凹域到凹域三种组合。障碍条件以凸、凹、半弱凸生成元的分解形式表达，并辅以关于ECH指标（ECH index）和作用的组合约束。

Result: 恢复了多圆盘嵌入柱面并的锐利障碍（sharp obstruction）；获得了某些四边形奇形域嵌入球和椭球的锐利结果；并证明了这些新障碍在若干情形下严格强于仅依赖ECH容量的判据。

Conclusion: 本文系统扩展了基于ECH的辛嵌入障碍理论，覆盖了更广类型的奇形域嵌入问题，并在多个具体嵌入场景中取得了最优结果，表明推广后的ECH方法比单一的ECH容量方法更具判别力。

Abstract: We study symplectic embeddings between four-dimensional toric domains using embedded contact homology. Extending Hutchings' criterion for embeddings between convex toric domains, we obtain obstructions for embeddings from convex toric domains into concave toric domains, from semi-weakly convex toric domains into convex toric domains, and from concave toric domains into concave toric domains. The obstructions are formulated in terms of factorizations of convex, concave, and semi-weakly convex generators together with combinatorial constraints relating their ECH indices and actions. As applications, we recover the sharp obstruction for symplectic embeddings of a polydisk into a union of cylinders, and obtain sharp results for embeddings of certain quadrilateral toric domains into balls and ellipsoids. We also show that these obstructions are in some cases strictly stronger than those coming only from ECH capacities.

</details>

### [73] [On the Formality of Configuration Spaces of $\mathbb{R}^{n'} \times \mathbb{C}^{n}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28200)

TL;DR: 本文完整分类了 $\mathbb{R}^{n'} \times \mathbb{C}^{n}$ 上构型空间的形变性，通过构造可构的 de Rham-Dolbeault 上同调理论给出 CDGA 模型，证明当 $(n'=0,n\ge2)$ 或 $(n'=1,n\ge1)$ 时非形式化，当 $n'\ge2,n\ge1$ 时通过可容许图与正则化积分建立显式拟同构实现形式化，并应用于拓扑-全纯场论的局部算子代数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构型空间的形变性是代数拓扑与数学物理中的核心问题。对于 $\mathbb{R}^n$ 的构型空间，其 CDGA 模型的形式化性质已有经典结果，但对混合维度空间 $\mathbb{R}^{n'} \times \mathbb{C}^{n}$ 缺乏完整分类。本文旨在填补这一空白，并建立与拓扑-全纯场论中局部算子代数结构的联系。

Method: 作者定义了可构的 de Rham-Dolbeault 上同调理论，为 $\Conf_m(\mathbb{R}^{n'} \times \mathbb{C}^{n})$ 构造可构 CDGA 模型。对形式化情形 $(n'\ge2,n\ge1)$，利用可容许图的图论 CDGA 与正则化构型空间积分，构造 CDGA 与其上同调之间的显式拟同构。

Result: 给出完整分类：当 $(n'=0,n\ge2)$ 或 $(n'=1,n\ge1)$ 时构型空间非形式化；当 $n'\ge2,n\ge1$ 时构型空间形式化。作为应用，证明 $\mathbb{R}^{n'} \times \mathbb{C}^{n}$ 上拓扑-全纯场论的局部算子代数同伦等价于高维顶点代数类比。

Conclusion: 本文完成了混合双曲-复结构空间中构型空间形变性的完整分类，揭示了维度组合对形式化性质的本质影响，并建立了形式化结果与高维顶点代数（拓扑-全纯场论局部结构）之间的深层联系。

Abstract: This paper presents a complete classification of the formality of configuration spaces of $\mathbb{R}^{n'} \times \mathbb{C}^{n}$. We define a constructible de Rham-Dolbeault cohomology theory which provides a constructible CDGA (commutative differential graded algebra) model of $\Conf_m(\mathbb{R}^{n'} \times \mathbb{C}^{n})$. For $(n'=0,n\ge2)$ or $(n'=1,n\ge1)$, the CDGAs are non-formal. For $n'\ge2,n\ge1$, we establish an explicit quasi-isomorphism between the constructible CDGA and its cohomology by using a diagrammatic CDGA of admissible diagrams and a regularized configuration space integral, which leads to the formality. As an application, we show that the local operator algebra of a topological-holomorphic field theory on $\mathbb{R}^{n'} \times \mathbb{C}^{n}$ ($n'\ge2,n\ge1$) is homotopically equivalent to a higher dimensional analog of vertex algebras.

</details>


### [74] [About the contractibility of the walking coinductive equivalence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28355)
*Viktoriya Ozornova,Martina Rovelli,Tashi Walde*

TL;DR: 本文研究walking coinductive equivalence的Roberts-Street神经所得到的带标记单纯集，证明它是一个非可缩的饱和complicial集，但其所有有限截断都是可缩的。作为右$(\infty,\infty)$-范畴的模型，它给出了一个具体例子：本身非可缩，但反射到左$(\infty,\infty)$-范畴后是可缩的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望找到一个具体的、明确的例子来说明右$(\infty,\infty)$-范畴与左$(\infty,\infty)$-范畴之间的差异，特别是通过反射构造展示这种差异的非平凡性。

Method: 文章考虑了walking coinductive equivalence的Roberts-Street神经，这是一个带标记的单纯集。作者通过分析其作为饱和complicial集的性质，研究其有限截断的收缩性，并利用饱和complicial集作为右$(\infty,\infty)$-范畴模型这一框架，构造出一个非平凡的例子。

Result: 证明了该Roberts-Street神经是一个非可缩的饱和complicial集，但其所有有限截断均是可缩的。该对象对应的右$(\infty,\infty)$-范畴本身非可缩，但其反射到左$(\infty,\infty)$-范畴后是平凡的（可缩的）。

Conclusion: 这提供了一个具体且显式的例子，说明右$(\infty,\infty)$-范畴到左$(\infty,\infty)$-范畴的反射可能会丢失信息，即使该信息在所有有限层次上都是不可见的。该例子对于理解左、右$(\infty,\infty)$-范畴模型之间的根本差异具有理论意义。

Abstract: We study the marked simplicial set obtained as the Roberts-Street nerve of the walking coinductive equivalence. Abstract: In this thesis, we study the spectral properties of dynamically defined aperiodic metric and discrete graphs. Our goal is to determine to what extent spectral properties of discrete one-dimensional ergodic Schrödinger operators persist when the aperiodicity is manifested through the geometry rather than through a potential. The graphs considered here are inspired by one-dimensional aperiodic tilings, and are called tiling graphs and decorated $\mathbb{Z}$-graphs.

For decorated $\mathbb{Z}$-graphs, we further show that for a Baire-generic and Lebesgue almost-sure choice of the decoration edge lengths, the spectrum is a generalized Cantor set.

We prove a gap labelling theorem, which characterizes the set of possible values taken by the IDS inside spectral gaps. We show that the gap labels are contained in the Schwartzman group associated with the dynamical system generating the graph, up to a geometric scaling factor.
  Lastly, we consider the Dry Ten Martini Problem for discrete Sturmian decorated $\mathbb{Z}$-graphs, asking whether all possible values predicted by the gap labelling theorem are indeed attained by the IDS inside spectral gaps. We answer this question negatively, by identifying a large set of gap labels which are not attained due to jump discontinuities of the IDS. We then show that away from these jump discontinuities, the periodic approximants for Sturmian graphs display the same combinatorial structure as the standard Sturmian Hamiltonians, and use this to obtain an explicit characterization of the realized gap labels for Sturmian comb graphs.

Abstract: Exact cumulants of entanglement entropies of random state ensembles have traditionally been studied within the random matrix framework. The approach also suggests a possible route towards unified and more efficient higher-order cumulant calculations by exploring integrable hierarchies.

Abstract: The lattice Green's functions of the cubic lattices are classical: each is a symmetric square of a second-order operator, with a closed form in complete elliptic integrals. The hyperkagome lattice, realized by the iridium sublattice of the spin-liquid candidate Na4Ir3O8, has resisted such a treatment: Varma and Monien reduced its density of states to a threefold integral they found no way to solve exactly. From exact lattice moments we obtain an irreducible third-order linear differential (Picard-Fuchs) operator annihilating the Green's function. It is not a literal symmetric square, yet its symmetric square carries a rational solution: the differential Galois group is orthogonal, and an order-two differential intertwiner makes the operator projectively a symmetric square. Our main result is that the underlying second-order operator is the uniformizing equation of the genus-zero modular curve of Gamma_0(30)+, the level-thirty group generated by Gamma_0(30) and all its Atkin-Lehner involutions, an explicit eta quotient parametrizing the natural variable. That uniformizing equation is itself not new - it is a row of the Lian-Yau table of genus-zero groups - so what is new here is the identification of a lattice Green's function with it, together with a proof: the Schwarzian identity is established by an a priori pole-degree bound rather than checked numerically, which also proves the tabulated entry. The hyperkagome Green's function is therefore modular at level thirty, supplying the closed form Varma and Monien sought. Its weight-two period is obtained explicitly: a depth-one quasimodular form which we prove is not a modular form times an algebraic function at any weight.

Abstract: We study a two-particle lattice Schrödinger operator describing two identical fermions on the one-dimensional lattice $\mathbb Z$ with nearest-neighbor interaction of strength $λ\in\mathbb R$. [4-4\cos(k/2),\,4+4\cos(k/2)]. \] We give a complete description of the spectral transition at both edges. The operator has a unique simple eigenvalue outside the essential spectrum if and only if \[ |λ|>2\cos(k/2), \] in which case \[ E(k,λ) =
4+λ+\frac{4\cos^2(k/2)}λ. \] For $|λ|<2\cos(k/2)$ there is no discrete eigenvalue. At the critical coupling $|λ|=2\cos(k/2)$, the eigenvalue merges with the corresponding spectral edge and becomes a threshold resonance, with a bounded non-square-integrable odd solution. We also derive the threshold and strong-coupling asymptotics of the eigenvalue and eigenfunction. In the strong-coupling regime the eigenfunction localizes at the interaction sites, whereas at critical coupling it converges pointwise to the corresponding resonant solution. The exceptional fiber $k=π$, where the hopping vanishes and the essential spectrum collapses to $\{4\}$, is treated separately.

</details>

Abstract: We consider a class of discrete multi-time determinantal point processes whose correlation kernels admit a double-contour integral form, containing Schur processes as simple examples. Method: 论文受Brockett-Wegner双交换子流启发，该流是非线性微分方程，控制（此处无界）算子的演化。作者对由于流不闭合而产生的非闭合代数结构进行详细分析，并显式地以自旋-玻色子耦合强度为参数界定高阶误差项，从而建立近似的严格对角化框架。

Result: 该工作为理论物理文献中广泛采用的小耦合区间的几种启发式对角化技术提供了首个严格的数学证明（在最简单的非平凡情形下），并使基于流算法的系统化高阶对角化和自能重整化成为可行方案。

Abstract: Spin-boson models are paradigmatic examples of open quantum systems and serve as the theoretical paradigm for current quantum computers based on single-ion trap technology. Despite their ubiquity, a complete spectral diagonalization of these models remains an open problem, except in highly singular regimes. This paper establishes a rigorous framework for the approximate diagonalization of generalized spin-boson systems. Our approach is inspired by the Brockett-Wegner double-commutator flow, which is a non-linear differential equation governing the evolution of (here unbounded) operators. Unlike relatively recent applications of this flow to quadratic Hamiltonians in quantum field theory, it does not close in the spin-boson context. We overcome this fundamental obstruction by performing a detailed analysis of the resulting non-closed algebraic structure, allowing us to explicitly bound the higher-order error term with respect to the spin-boson coupling strength. Consequently, this work provides the first mathematically rigorous justification for several heuristic diagonalization techniques widely employed in the theoretical physics literature for small-coupling regimes, in the simplest non-trivial cases. More broadly, our framework renders a flow-based algorithm feasible for systematic higher-order diagonalization and self-energy renormalization. This strategy is conceptually akin to multi-scale analysis, or, much more recently, to the iterative, local Lie-Schwinger block-diagonalization method by Fröhlich and Pizzo.

</details>

*Xiuqing Duan*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gabrielov-Novikov-Shapiro提出了关于逆幂势在直线上临界点数量的有符号直线猜想，该猜想此前仅在更窄的范围内成立；Edelsbrunner-Fillmore-Oliveira也提出了关于正电荷配置的猜想3。本文旨在证明/推广这两个猜想，以完整刻画投影逆幂势的零点结构。

Method: 本文的核心结构输入是一个投影配对Haar定理（projective paired Haar theorem）：对有限β>1，由2m维空间ΣL_j/Q_j^β（其中Q_j为两两不成比例的正定二元二次型，L_j为任意实线性分子）中的函数至多有2m-1个带重数的投影零点。在此基础上，通过将有限逆幂势按投影中心和平方高度(a,b²)合并归类，利用该Haar定理证明二分法结论。

Result: 证明了以下二分法定理：(1) 若m=0（有效高度类全部消失），精确抵消当且仅当每类系数和为零，且原域的所有普通点均为临界点；(2) 若m≥1，至多有2m-1个临界点。此结果证明了Gabrielov-Novikov-Shapiro猜想对所有α>0成立，并通过p=2α的替换证明了Edelsbrunner-Fillmore-Oliveira猜想3对所有p>0成立。此外，对每个n构造了一个显式正n电荷配置恰好达到2n-1个简单临界点，说明该界是紧的。

Conclusion: 本文完整解决了Gabrielov-Novikov-Shapiro的有符号直线猜想（对所有α>0）并推广了Edelsbrunner-Fillmore-Oliveira猜想3到所有p>0的范围，同时给出了达到极值的显式构型，表明2m-1（或2n-1）的临界点界是最优的。该结果为投影逆幂势的临界点理论研究提供了完整且紧的刻画。

Abstract: Let \(α>0\) and restrict a finite inverse-power potential with arbitrary real coefficients to a line. Combine terms having the same projected centre and squared height \((a,b^2)\), delete classes whose coefficient sum is zero, and let \(m\) be the number of remaining classes. We prove the following dichotomy. If \(m=0\), exact cancellation occurs if and only if every class sum vanishes, and every ordinary point of the original domain is critical. If \(m\geq1\), there are at most \(2m-1\) critical points: when all effective heights are positive the zeros are counted with analytic multiplicity, while in the presence of effective sources on the line the assertion is one global distinct-point bound. This proves the signed line conjecture of Gabrielov--Novikov--Shapiro, valid throughout their range and in fact for every \(α>0\). The structural input is a projective paired Haar theorem: for finite \(β>1\), the full \(2m\)-dimensional space \(\sum L_j/Q_j^β\), with pairwise nonproportional positive-definite binary quadratics and arbitrary real linear numerators, has at most \(2m-1\) projective zeros counted with multiplicity. For positive charges, the substitution \(p=2α\) proves Conjecture~3 of Edelsbrunner--Fillmore--Oliveira throughout its stated range \(p\geq1\) and extends the same conclusion to every \(p>0\). For every \(n\), an explicit positive \(n\)-charge configuration attains \(2n-1\) simple critical points.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sasakian流形是Kähler流形的奇数维对应物，但其上的Dolbeault型算子的性质远不如Kähler情形清楚。作者的目标是将Kähler几何中著名的Kähler恒等式推广到Sasakian流形，从而理解Dolbeault Laplacian与Hodge Laplacian之间的精确关系，并利用这种关系获得谱估计。

Method: 在Sasakian流形的全微分形式复形上构建两对一阶Dolbeault型算子（推广Kohn-Rossi微分），建立Kähler型恒等式。将Dolbeault Laplacian与Hodge Laplacian的关系明确表述（含Reeb流产生的额外项），并借助Lefschetz分解对特征值进行上下界估计。

Result: 得到了Dolbeault Laplacian与Hodge Laplacian之间的显式关系（含Reeb流修正项），并据此导出了Hodge Laplacian在微分形式上的上下特征值估计，这些估计以Reeb向量场的Lie导数特征值刻画。

Conclusion: Sasakian流形上的Hodge Laplacian谱结构比Kähler情形更丰富，Reeb流的作用是关键因素。通过Kähler型恒等式与Lefschetz分解，给出了依赖于Reeb流特征值的谱估计，深化了对Sasakian流形Hodge理论的理解。

Abstract: Sasakian manifolds, the odd-dimensional analogues of Kähler manifolds, carry two natural pairs of first-order Dolbeault-type operators on the full complex of differential forms, extending the Kohn--Rossi differentials. On such Sasaki manifolds, we establish Kähler-type identities for these operators, and relate the resulting Dolbeault Laplacians to the Hodge Laplacian. Unlike the Kähler case, where $Δ=2Δ_{\overline\partial}$, the relation has extra terms coming from the Reeb flow. Using these formulas and a Lefschetz decomposition, we derive lower and upper eigenvalue estimates for $Δ$ on forms depending on the eigenvalues of the Lie derivative of the Reeb vector field.

</details>

Abstract: We establish flow and homotopy tools for Banach Lie algebroids that do not rely on compactly supported extensions. Under suitable hypotheses, we construct parameter-dependent local extensions of sections along algebroid paths, complete lifts and their fibrewise-linear evolutions, and a Bochner-integral variation-of-constants formula. We then prove that an admissible-path homotopy is equivalently a Lie algebroid morphism from the parameter square and a solution of the associated transport equation. This provides the extension, transport and infinitesimal variation theory required for a local integration program of Banach Lie algebroids to be developed in a sequel.

</details>

Result: 在正Yamabe不变量Y(M,[g])>0、正Yamabe型Q-曲率不变量Y₄*(M,[g])>0以及额外初始度量条件的假设下，证明了保形类[g]中存在一个度量，其数量曲率和Q-曲率同时为正。

Conclusion: 该结果表明，在5维情形下，结合两个正曲率不变量条件并附加初始度量条件，可以通过连续性方法在保形类内找到同时具有正数量曲率和正Q-曲率的度量，这为高维流形上双重正曲率度量的存在性提供了新的理论依据。

Abstract: Suppose $(M, g)$ is a smooth, closed, $5$-dimensional Riemannian manifold with positive Yamabe invariant $Y(M, [g]) > 0$ and positive Yamabe-type $Q$-curvature invariant $Y_{4}^{\ast}(M, [g]) > 0$. Using the continuity method, we prove the existence of a metric in the conformal class $[g]$ with positive scalar curvature and positive $Q$-curvature, assuming an additional condition on an ``initial metric" in the class $[g]$.

</details>

Abstract: The reduction of mechanical presymplectic structures and its application to the reduction of time-dependent Hamiltonian systems were developed in a recent paper (Gutiérrez Sagredo et al, 2025). In this paper we will describe the reduction of time-dependent Hamiltonian systems using the extended formalism. This process combines cotangent bundle reduction with reduction of presymplectic structures of corank 1 and 2. These results generalize previous work (Lacirasella et al, 2012), in the same direction. Several examples will be presented to illustrate the theory from this new perspective.

Abstract: Let $M^n$ be a $C^2$ closed convex hypersurface in Euclidean space, and $σ_m$ be its $m$th mean curvature. Abstract: We propose boundary theories, CFT$_{p}$ with $p\in β\mathbb{N}/\mathbb{N}$, which can be interpreted as an ensemble of theories in the context of AdS/CFT duality, when the boundary spacetime dimension is more than one. These theories are only emergent in the infinite $N$ limit of the boundary CFT and depend on $p$, which corresponds to the different ways one can take a generalized form of the infinite $N$ limit, in the presence of a chaotic or an oscillatory operator. This generalized infinite $N$ limit is called an \emph{ultralimit}, and $p$ is a \emph{free ultrafilter} on $\mathbb{N}$. We propose that the gravitational path integral computes an average of CFT$_{p}$ in each of its sectors; and in the appropriate cases, it produces baby universes and spacetime wormholes. We apply this proposal to the Antonini-Sasieta-Swingle (AS$^{2}$) like states.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将MERA扩展到相互作用量子场论时，非高斯纠缠器（entangler）的计算处理面临重大挑战。自然的策略是采用BCH展开在有限阶终止的多项式纠缠器，但该策略是否足以捕捉正确的普适类（特别是非均场临界行为）尚不清楚，因此需要系统分析这一族纠缠器的表达力极限。

Method: 在二维相互作用的φ⁴量子场论的wavelet MERA（wMERA）框架下，对有限BCH多项式纠缠器族进行解析分析：通过计算其生成的有效势的一般形式，判断是否必为Landau形式；同时对非一般（退化）情形单独分析。数值上，系统地变化纠缠器的非局域范围和变分复杂度，计算临界指数β并追踪其行为趋势。

Result: 解析结果表明：在一般情况下，有限BCH多项式纠缠器产生的有效势必然具有Landau形式，确立整个类别的均场普适性；在非一般的退化情形下，临界指数偏离均场值但无法达到Ising值β=1/8。数值实验中，所有考虑的ansätze的临界指数β均与均场值1/2一致，增大非局域范围或变分复杂度并未使其向Ising值漂移。

Conclusion: 有限BCH多项式纠缠器存在代数障碍，无法复现非均场临界性。该类纠缠器天然受限于均场普适类，这为MERA在相互作用量子场论中的应用设定了一个根本性限制。要正确捕获Ising普适类等非均场临界行为，需要跳出多项式/有限BCH框架，考虑非多项式或无穷BCH纠缠器构造。

Abstract: The computational treatment of non-Gaussian entanglers could pose a significant challenge when extending the Multi-Scale Entanglement Renormalization Ansatz (MERA) to interacting quantum field theories. A natural strategy is therefore to consider polynomial entanglers for which the Baker-Campbell-Hausdorff (BCH) expansion terminates at finite order. In this work, we identify an algebraic obstruction that limits the universality classes accessible to this family of entanglers. Working within the wavelet MERA (wMERA) framework applied to the interacting $φ^4$ theory in two dimensions, we show analytically that the effective potential generated by any finite-BCH polynomial entangler is necessarily of Landau form in the generic case, establishing mean-field universality for the full class; in non-generic, degenerate cases the resulting exponent departs from mean-field but still fails to reproduce the Ising value. As a numerical illustration, the critical exponent $β$ remains consistent with its mean-field value $β= 1/2$ across all ansätze considered, with no drift toward the Ising value $β= 1/8$ as the nonlocality range or variational complexity increases. Reproducing non-mean-field criticality therefore might require non-polynomial or infinite-BCH constructions.

</details>

Abstract: We compute high frequency gravitational wave radiation in shockwave collisions of a light mass compact object with energy $μ_L$ off a heavy compact object (black hole) with energy $μ_H \gg μ_L$. A formal solution is obtained in terms of shockwave propagators that provide snapshots of $O(μ_Lμ_H^n)$ tidal response functions sensitive to classical and potentially semi-classical dynamics of black hole formation. In a perturbative expansion, the first nontrivial contribution of $O(μ_Lμ_H)$ is the emission of a graviton from the Lipatov vertex generated by the fusion of two reggeized gravitons. For graviton frequencies $ω\rightarrow 0$, the Lipatov amplitude reduces to the ultrarelativistic limit of the single-graviton exchange Weinberg amplitude. At $O(μ_Lμ_H^2)$, the Lipatov amplitude includes a piece accounting for the rescattering of the graviton off a reggeized graviton and another, corresponding to graviton emission from a vertex fusing three reggeized gravitons. These nontrivial contributions again simplify to the Weinberg radiative amplitude for two-graviton exchange in the soft limit. We highlight throughout double copy structures to gluon radiation in dilute-dense collisions of Yang-Mills shockwaves. We relate our shockwave results to the eikonal multiple scattering framework of Veneziano et al.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为de Sitter时空中的宇宙学Yang-Mills关联函数提供几何化描述，从而为更高点的宇宙学关联函数建立可推广的几何框架，类似于平坦空间中振幅的散射振幅正几何程序。

Method: 利用宇宙学Grassmannian上的螺旋度剥离表示（helicity-stripped representatives），通过Pfaffian（或旋量）嵌入将物理奇点转化为自然几何边界。在三点情形采用非负象限的规范形式；在四点情形利用Mandelstam除子对Pfaffian正域进行划分，并通过因子化条件和平坦空间极限唯一确定定向并集或同调环的符号线性组合。

Result: 三点YM关联函数对应Grassmannian中非负象限的规范形式；四点的约化色序关联函数由两个正几何区域的定向并集给出，完整色序关联函数则由同调环的符号线性组合唯一确定。

Conclusion: 正几何方法能够有效捕获de Sitter中三点和四点的YM关联函数，但完整四点结果需要更广义的同调表述。该工作为更高点宇宙学关联函数的几何描述提供了具体起点，表明同调层面的正几何框架是必要的。

Abstract: We develop a positive-geometric formulation of tree-level Yang-Mills correlators in de Sitter space at three and four points through their helicity-stripped representatives on the cosmological Grassmannian. In its Pfaffian (or spinor) embedding, physical singularities become natural geometric boundaries. At three points, the Yang-Mills correlator is the canonical form of the non-negative orthant in the Grassmannian. At four points, the Mandelstam divisors partition the Pfaffian-positive domain of the Grassmannian into four positive geometries. Requiring factorization into three-point forms, together with the correct flat-space limit, uniquely selects an oriented union of two of these regions, whose canonical form reproduces the reduced color-ordered Yang-Mills correlator. The full color-ordered correlator, on the other hand, arises from a uniquely fixed signed linear combination of homology cycles. Thus, the broader homological formulation of positive geometry is essential for capturing the complete four-point result. Our construction provides a concrete starting point for a geometric description of higher-point cosmological correlators.

</details>


### [97] [The Late-time Ramp of the Double-Scaled SYK Model from the large-$n$ tail of Cactus Diagram](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27627)
*Yao Li*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文从仙人掌图（cactus diagrams）出发，推导了双标度SYK模型中有限温度谱形因子的late-time线性斜坡（ramp），直接证明了其与随机矩阵理论的预言一致，并给出了温度依赖性和对称因子的微观起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单迹可观测量可以用弦图（chord diagram）精确描述，但多迹求和受到弦交叉权重q_IJ的阻碍（它们不能独立平均为q）。作者希望解决这一困难，直接从微观尺度推导谱形因子的ramp行为，建立q-变形量子代数框架下ramp的微观起源。

Result: 得到Z_s^{sing}(β+it,β-it)=s_p c_N |t|/(2π) ∫ e^{-2βE}dE + O(1)，其中对称因子s_p在4|p时为2、否则为1。该结果复现了随机矩阵理论的线性ramp预言，包括温度依赖性和对称因子，并与半经典预言一致。

Conclusion: 该工作为双标度SYK模型中ramp行为提供了直接的微观推导，其关键洞见是ramp的非解析贡献由仙人掌图展开的大阶尾部控制，对低阶解析项的变化不敏感。plateau区域超出了当前分析范围，留待后续研究。

Abstract: We derive the late-time ramp of the finite-temperature spectral form factor in the double-scaled SYK model directly from cactus diagrams, which is introduced as a multi-trace generalization of chord diagram in Ref \cite{Berkooz:2020fvm}. Although single-trace observables admit an exact chord-diagram description, multi-trace sums are obstructed by the chord-intersection weights $q_{IJ}$, which cannot be averaged independently to $q$. Our key idea is that the non-analytic contribution responsible for the ramp is controlled by the large-order tail of the Cactus-diagram expansion and is insensitive to finite changes in its low-order analytic terms. Decomposing the contribution with $n$-cross-trace-pairings Cactus diagram into two buds $B_n$ and a kernel $\mathcal K_n$, we determine their large-$n$ asymptotics with fixed $0\leq q<1$ and resum the resulting tail. For $β_L=β+it$ and $β_R=β-it$, we obtain \begin{equation*} Z_s^{\mathrm{sing}}(β+it,β-it) =s_p c_N \frac{|t|}{2π} \int_{E_{min}}^{E_{max}} e^{-2βE} dE+O(1), \qquad s_p=\begin{cases}2,&4\mid p,\\1,&4\nmid p.\end{cases} \end{equation*} This result reproduces the linear ramp predicted by random matrix theory, including its temperature dependence and symmetry factor, and agrees with the semiclassical predictions. It provides a direct microscopic origin of the ramp within the general $q$-deformed quantum algebra. While the plateau lies beyond the scope of the present analysis and calculation.

</details>

Abstract: We establish that the entanglement entropy of the electromagnetic vacuum, when regulated by weak spacetime curvature, imposes a fundamental lower bound on the radial resolution available to quantum excitations. Starting from the area law of vacuum entanglement, we demonstrate how linearized gravity introduces a natural ultraviolet regulator via the perturbative Green's function. An exact geometric projection from tangential to radial resolution yields a minimal radial scale $Δr_{\min} \propto r_s^2/R$, where $r_s$ is the Schwarzschild radius of the enclosing body and $R$ is the boundary radius.

Measured particle masses satisfy $m = αm_{\text{geo}}$, where $α$ is a dimensionless factor encoding the vacuum's informational structure. For Earth, $m_{\text{geo}} \approx 2.84 \times 10^{-32}$ kg.
  We determine $α$ from first principles via spectral analysis of the curvature-induced mode density shift, implemented using a Python framework with $N=3000$ radial grid points and $n_{\text{modes}}=1000$. The calculation yields $α(2) \approx 33$, naturally matching the electron benchmark ($α\approx 32$) without adjustable parameters.
  This framework reframes mass as a probe of a vacuum-imposed resolution limit set by global geometry and entanglement. The vacuum structure itself forbids measurement of classical particles lighter than the electron in terrestrial laboratories, establishing entanglement entropy a measurable signature of quantum vacuum structure.

TL;DR: 本文推广了"转折反常维度"（cusp anomalous dimension）的概念至平面共形边界或界面存在下的转角线缺陷，研究了由三个角度及转角到边界距离刻画的几何构型，推导了对数系数的新约束并验证于多种理论。

Result: 推导出短距离（紫外）和长距离（红外）两个对数项的系数约束，验证了角向Hessian负半定性条件。在全息强耦合计算中发现由转角相对界面的几何驱动的Gross-Ooguri型相变，并揭示了该设置中转折自由能与全息转角纠缠熵之间的密切联系。

Conclusion: 共形边界/界面下的转折反常维度存在新的长程对数贡献，这些系数受到反射正定性导出的严格约束，并在解析上可验证的弱耦合与强耦合例子中均成立；全息结果进一步展示了几何驱动的相变及与纠缠熵的有趣联系。

Abstract: The cusp anomalous dimension is the universal logarithmic contribution to the free energy of a line defect with a cusp, and provides a useful observable characterizing defect dynamics. We generalize this notion to cusped line defects in the presence of a planar conformal boundary or interface. The geometry is characterized by three angles, and the distance of the cusp from the boundary or interface which separates short-distance and long-distance regimes. The resulting free energy contains two large logarithmic terms: a short-distance contribution governed by the usual vacuum cusp anomalous dimension, and a new long-distance contribution sensitive to the boundary or interface. Using reflection positivity for transverse deformations of the defect rays, we derive several constraints on the coefficients of these logarithmic terms, which can be packaged into negative semi-definiteness of their angular Hessians. We also derive constraints on the continuation of these coefficients to Lorentzian kinematics. We compute these coefficients and verify the constraints explicitly in free scalar and Maxwell theories and in planar $\mathcal N=4$ super-Yang-Mills theory at weak coupling for various choices of boundary conditions. At strong coupling, we compute the logarithmic coefficients in the holographic D3-D5 defect CFT and find a Gross-Ooguri-like phase transition between competing string worldsheets driven by the geometry of the cusp relative to the interface. We also observe a close connection of the cusp free energy in this setup with holographic corner entanglement entropy.

</details>

Method: 通过对受保护标量算符两点函数进行双圈计算，识别出积分的超越性结构，推导出通用有限秩公式，并利用Young格上的核函数及平面极限的划分理论简化来分析结果。

Conclusion: 研究为ABJM理论中受保护两点函数的一致超越性提供了证据，建立了与树级度量及Young格核函数的深层联系，并为该理论的量子修正结构提供了统一框架。

Abstract: We consider two-loop corrections to two-point functions of protected scalar operators in ABJM theory. We infer a compact finite-rank formula valid for operators of arbitrary dimension and multi-trace structure. The result is governed by the exact tree-level metric and a simple kernel on the Young lattice, while the planar limit reduces to a simple partition-theoretic rule. The required integrals organize into uniformly transcendental combinations, providing evidence for uniform transcendentality of these protected two-point functions.

</details>


### [101] [Self-Dual Yang-Mills in AdS$_4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27731)

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索AdS4中Yang-Mills理论在复杂耦合（含θ角）参数空间中的新极限行为，特别是自对偶极限下理论与BF表述之间的等价性，以及θ角在AdS中与平直空间完全不同的物理效应。

Method: 通过定义自对偶极限g_-→0（g_+固定），利用微扰理论论证极限理论良好定义性；结合BF表述和边界条件（关联B与F）建立等价性；通过树级边界关联函数的计算验证理论的物理性质，并在平直空间极限下检验与已知胶子振幅的一致性。

Result: 证明了自对偶极限理论与自对偶Yang-Mills的BF表述等价；g_+作为圈计数参数；所有树级零加和单加边界关联函数消失；给出了双加树级关联函数的封闭形式表达式；平直空间极限下总能量极点的约化正确复现了单负胶子振幅。

Conclusion: AdS4中的自对偶Yang-Mills理论是一个在微扰层面良定义且结构丰富的理论，其θ角在AdS背景中具有非平凡的物理效应（与平直空间形成鲜明对比），并且通过AdS/CFT对应有望对偶到具有自对偶结构的边界理论。

Abstract: Quantum Yang-Mills theory in Euclidean AdS$_4$ with Neumann boundary conditions is studied as a function of the complex couplings $\frac{1}{g_\pm^2} \equiv \frac{1}{g^2}\mp \frac{i θ}{8 π^2}$. We define a "self-dual limit" by $g_- \to 0$ with $g_+ ~{\rm fixed}$. We argue that the limiting theory is well-defined perturbatively and equivalent to the BF formulation of self-dual Yang-Mills theory with a particular boundary condition relating $B$ and $F$ at the boundary $\partial$AdS$_4$. $g_+$ is the loop-counting parameter of the self-dual theory. This is in stark contrast to flat space, where $θ$ has no effect on perturbative dynamics. In the self-dual limit, it is shown that all tree-level zero-plus and single-plus boundary correlators vanish. A closed form expression is given for any number of gluons for the double-plus tree correlators. Finally, we show that in the flat space limit of AdS$_4$, the total energy poles in the tree boundary correlators correctly reduce to the single-minus gluon amplitudes.

</details>


### [102] [Seiberg dualities for quiver gauge theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27803)
*Yuanyuan Fang,Jing Feng,Dan Xie*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了包含基本表示、双基本表示和秩二代数张量物质的箭图规范理论的Seiberg对偶，通过矩阵Hilbert级数的因式分解方程推导对偶约束，并从大N超共形指标的闭式解出发，恢复了两节点箭图中的SU-SU和SO-USp乘积群对偶，同时讨论了有限N情形下的反常匹配和算子谱匹配一致性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Seiberg对偶是理解强耦合规范场论非微扰行为的重要工具，但对于包含基本表示、双基本表示和秩二代数张量物质的箭图规范理论，其对偶结构的系统性刻画仍然缺乏。作者旨在通过对无裹挞介子（undressed mesons）谱的约束，建立对偶存在性的判据，并为更广泛的箭图理论提供统一的分析框架。

Method: 作者首先指出Seiberg对偶的存在性对无裹挞介子谱施加了强约束，该约束可自然编码为矩阵Hilbert级数的因式分解方程。随后，他们从一般箭图规范理论的大N超共形指标的闭式表达式出发，推导出该因式分解。将该方法应用于两节点箭图，并恢复已知的SU-SU与SO-USp乘积群对偶。此外，还通过反常匹配和算子谱匹配讨论了有限N的一致性条件。

Result: 成功推导了矩阵Hilbert级数因式分解方程与大N超共形指标之间的联系，并在两节点箭图情形下恢复了已知的SU-SU与SO-USp Seiberg对偶对。同时给出了有限N下反常匹配和算子谱匹配的一致性验证。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一种系统的、基于矩阵Hilbert级数因式分解的Seiberg对偶判定方法，适用于包含基本、双基本和秩二代数张量物质的一般箭图规范理论，为更广泛的乘积群对偶探索和有限N一致性检验奠定了方法论基础。

Abstract: We study Seiberg duality for quiver gauge theories with fundamental, bifundamental, and rank-two tensor matter. The existence of a Seiberg dual description places strong constraints on the spectrum of undressed mesons, which is naturally encoded in a factorization equation for the matrix Hilbert series. We derive this factorization from a closed-form large-$N$ superconformal index for general quiver gauge theories with these matter contents. We apply the method to two-node quivers and recover known $SU$--$SU$ and $SO$--$USp$ product-group dualities. We also discuss finite-$N$ consistency conditions from anomaly and operator-spectrum matching.

</details>


### [103] [Superconformal index of large N and long quivers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27933)

Method: 提出了一个通用框架，用于在大N极限下计算quivergauge理论的生成函数。将该方法应用于4d N=2超共形指标和5d N=1理论的0-瞬子指标，同时处理有限quiver和长quiver极限，涵盖特殊酉群和正交辛群等规范群。

Result: 该框架展示了广泛的适用性；在Hall-Littlewood极限下，所得的级数展开捕获了预期的Higgs分支手性环生成元；长quiver极限的精确表达式为全息对偶的翘曲AdS5和AdS6背景提供了超引力指标的新预测。

Conclusion: 该通用框架在多个场景中验证了有效性，不仅提供了对指标的新见解，还为超引力指标提供了全新的预测，展示了其作为quivergauge理论大N分析工具的多功能性和强大潜力。

Abstract: We develop a universal framework for computing generating functions in quiver gauge theories in the large $N$ limit. We apply this method to the superconformal index of 4d $\mathcal{N}=2$ gauge theories, as well as to the 0-instanton index of 5d $\mathcal{N}=1$ theories, both for finite quivers of arbitrary shape and in the long quiver limit, including both special unitary and orthosymplectic gauge groups, demonstrating its versatility. In the Hall--Littlewood limit, we show that the series expansion of the resulting index captures the expected Higgs branch chiral ring generators. Our exact expressions in the long quiver limit provide new predictions for the supergravity index of the holographically dual warped AdS$_5$ and AdS$_6$ backgrounds.

</details>


### [104] [Holographic Ensembles in Type IIB](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27956)
*Jesse van Muiden*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了IIB型弦理论中Schur twisted AdS$_5 \times S^5$背景下的全息系综，及其对应的边界配分函数——$\mathcal{N}=4$ SYM的Schur指数。在超引力极限下存在两种自然系综：固定$S^5$通量（对应边界固定秩的规范系综）或固定AdS$_5$边界势（对应边界大正则系综）。由于IIB超引力中五形式场的自对偶性，系综选择无法在标准体作用量中体现，而是通过额外拓扑项的耦合实现。作者计算了单圈级别的体配分函数，包括Kaluza-Klein行列式和系综变化求和的测度，并证明根据拓扑项的符号，其结果分别与规范系综或大正则系综的微扰边界配分函数相匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解全息对偶中重整化群流或"c-定理"背景下的边界系综选择如何从体理论中涌现，特别是IIB超引力中由于五形式场自对偶性导致系综自由度在标准体作用量中不可见这一微妙问题。该工作旨在阐明体理论中系综选择和边界理论中系综选择之间的精确对应关系。

Method: 在Schur twisted AdS$_5 \times S^5$背景（对应$\mathcal{N}=4$ SYM的Schur指数）下，利用AdS/CFT对应关系计算体配分函数至单圈阶。方法包括：(1) 引入额外的拓扑项来编码系综选择（固定通量 vs 固定势）；(2) 计算Kaluza-Klein行列式；(3) 计算系综变化求和的测度；(4) 将体配分函数与规范系综和大正则系综下的微扰边界Schur指数进行对比验证。

Result: 结果表明，拓扑项的符号决定了系综的选择：一种符号对应边界规范系综（固定秩）的微扰配分函数，另一种符号对应大正则系综（固定化学势）的微扰配分函数。单圈计算（包含KK行列式与系综求和测度）精确匹配了两种边界系综的微扰展开。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在Schur指数的全息对偶中明确展示了体理论中的系综选择如何通过拓扑项编码，并精确验证了AdS/CFT中体-边界系综对应的机制。这一结果为理解全息系综更广义的结构（包括拓扑项在体理论中的作用）提供了重要范例。

Abstract: We study holographic ensembles in type IIB string theory on the Schur twisted AdS$_5 \times S^5$ background, and its corresponding boundary partition function: the Schur index of $\mathcal{N}=4$ SYM. In the supergravity limit the two natural ensembles are either at fixed flux on $S^5$, corresponding to a boundary theory at fixed rank, or its conjugate choice of fixed potential on the boundary of AdS$_5$, corresponding on the boundary to a grand canonical ensemble of theories at fixed chemical potential. Due to the self-dual nature of the five-form in type IIB supergravity the choice of ensembles is not seen in the standard bulk action, but rather by the coupling of an additional topological term. We compute the bulk partition function to one loop, including the Kaluza-Klein determinant and the measure of the ensemble-changing sum, and show that depending on the sign of this topological term it matches the perturbative boundary partition function in either the canonical or the grand canonical ensemble.

</details>


### [105] [Revisiting the $U(1)$-$CP$ tension in QCD from a 1PI-action perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28253)
*Paolo Di Vecchia,Francesco Sannino,Graham M. Shore,Gabriele Veneziano,Shimon Yankielowicz*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文讨论了在QCD框架内同时解决U(1)问题和强CP问题的内在矛盾。通过对规范不变的复合算符构造的1PI有效作用量Γ进行低能展开，并施加Ward恒等式约束后，作者证明在小的非零θ̄角下，只有当反常诱导的赝标量质量矩阵贡献远小于非反常部分（即U(1)问题未解决）时，才能避免CP破坏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QCD中存在两大经典难题：U(1)问题（η'介子质量为何如此之大）和强CP问题（为何θ̄角如此之小）。作者希望探究这两个问题在QCD本身框架内是否能够同时得到解决，或者两者之间存在根本性的张力。

Method: 作者选取一组适当的规范不变（复合）算符，构造1PI有效作用量Γ并考虑其低能展开。通过对Γ施加Ward恒等式约束，在少量假设条件下严格证明了CP守恒与小θ̄角的相容性条件，并引入拓扑磁化率作为关键物理量来量化这一张力。

Result: 证明了一个重要定理：在小的非零θ̄角下，只有反常诱导的赝标量质量矩阵贡献相对于非反常部分可完全忽略时，即U(1)问题未得到解决时，才能避免CP破坏。这揭示了U(1)问题的解决与强CP问题的避免之间存在不可避免的紧张关系。同时，QCD中的拓扑磁化率及其从纯Yang-Mills理论的精确推广成为确立这一张力和修正θ̄低阶结果的关键角色。

Conclusion: U(1)问题的解决与强CP问题的避免在QCD内部无法同时实现，二者存在本质张力。QCD的拓扑磁化率在这一张力中起核心作用，并驱动对θ̄线性阶已知结果的修正。

Abstract: We discuss the tension one encounters in trying to solve, simultaneously, the $U(1)$ and strong-$CP$ problems in QCD with light quarks and a small $\barθ$ angle within QCD itself. We do so by considering the low-energy expansion of a $1$PI effective action $Γ$, a functional of a properly chosen set of gauge-invariant (composite) operators. After enforcing Ward-identity constraints on $Γ$, we prove, under minor assumptions, that $CP$ violation at small non-vanishing $\barθ$ can be avoided only if the anomaly-induced contribution to the pseudoscalar mass matrix is completely negligible when compared to its non-anomalous counterpart - that is, only if the $U(1)$ problem is not solved. The topological susceptibility in QCD and a precise generalization of the one in pure Yang-Mills theory emerge as the most important players in establishing the above tension and in determining the corrections to various known results at leading order in $\barθ$.

</details>


### [106] [Scattering Equations as the lowest order K-identities in the calculation of Stringy Scaling of Hard String Scattering Amplitudes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28311)
*Sheng-Hong Lai,Jen-Chi Lee,Yi Yang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文明确证明了此前在一阶张量硬弦散射振幅（HSSA）计算中提出的n点K恒等式，这些恒等式是鞍点计算中弦性标度行为以及与零范数态退耦计算一致匹配的关键。此外，文章引入了一个G函数来生成无限集合的广义K恒等式（GKI），其中最低阶GKI对应于CHY形式论中场论振幅计算中使用的散射方程（SE），次低阶GKI即此前用于一阶张量HSSA计算的K恒等式，并推测更高阶GKI可用于计算更高阶HSSA及/或多张量HSSA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证此前提出的n点K恒等式，因为它们是一阶张量硬弦散射振幅计算中弦性标度行为和零范数态退耦一致性的关键；同时希望构建一个统一的生成框架（G函数），将散射方程、K恒等式等联系起来，以推广到更高阶和多张量HSSA的计算。

Method: 对先前提出的n点K恒等式进行显式数学证明；引入一个G函数作为生成函数，通过展开产生无限集合的广义K恒等式（GKI），并指出不同阶GKI分别对应现有框架中的散射方程（CHY形式论）和K恒等式（一阶张量HSSA）。

Result: 成功证明了n点K恒等式的正确性；建立了G函数生成的GKI层级结构，最低阶对应CHY散射方程，次低阶对应此前的K恒等式；基于此提出了更高阶/多张量HSSA计算的可扩展性猜想。

Conclusion: K恒等式得到了严格的数学证明，并通过G函数实现了广义化，建立了从散射方程到K恒等式的统一层级。该框架有望推广到更高阶弦散射振幅的计算，为弦理论与场论振幅之间的联系提供了更系统的数学结构。

Abstract: We prove explicitly the n-point K-identities we proposed previously in the calculation of one tensor hard string scattering amplitudes (HSSA). These K-identities were the key to obtain the stringy scaling behavior in the saddle point calculation of the n-point HSSA and, on the other hand, to consistently match with the calculation of decoupling of zero norm states. Moreover, we introduce a G function to generate an infinite set of generalized K-identities (GKI). The lowest order set of these GKI is the scattering equations (SE) used in the calculation of field theory amplitudes in the CHY formalism. The next to leading order set of these GKI is the K-identities used previously in the calculation of one tensor HSSA. We conjecture that the higher order sets of these GKI can be used to calculate higher order HSSA and/or multi-tensor HSSA.

</details>


### [107] [Stress-Testing Swampland Bounds with Class S Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28333)
*Gabriel Fenati,Miguel Montero,Irene Valenzuela*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过CFT对偶，在AdS5引力时空中检验了Sharpened Distance Conjecture（SharpDC）和Refined Distance Conjecture（RDC）。作者证明了对任何具有有限个规范因子和消失单圈β函数的大N拉格朗日超对称规范理论，SharpDC成立；但在大中心荷极限中该猜想并非普遍成立——在Class S理论中找到了M→∞、N固定的极限下违反SharpDC的具体例子。对于无孔的Class S理论，RDC被转化为模空间中Weil-Petersson直径的精确上界，该上界与现有数学文献一致但尚未被证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Distance Conjecture是弦论中关于无穷远处出现轻态指数塔的核心猜想，其精细版本（SharpDC和RDC）对质量衰减速率和场范围给出了更精确的约束。然而这些改良版猜想在具体引力背景（尤其是AdS）中的普适性尚不清楚，因此需要在AdS5引力时空中通过CFT对偶进行系统检验。

Method: 利用AdS/CFT对偶，将AdS5引力时空中的无穷远处共形流形极限映射为CFT共形流形上的对称性增强点。作者：(1) 对任意有限M个规范因子且β函数单圈为零的大N超对称规范理论，利用CFT数据证明SharpDC；(2) 通过构造Class S理论中的具体反例，检验SharpDC在大中心荷极限下的普适性；(3) 将无孔Class S理论中的RDC检验转化为对黎曼面模空间（亏格g）的Weil-Petersson直径的数学上界问题。

Result: (1) SharpDC对任何有限M的大N拉格朗日超对称规范理论（单圈β函数为零）成立。(2) 在Class S理论中，M→∞且N固定时，存在SharpDC被违反的具体例子，此时体内部几何尺寸远大于AdS曲率尺度，表明SharpDC并非在大中心荷极限下普遍成立。(3) RDC在Class S（无孔）大N极限下等价于对黎曼面模空间Weil-Petersson直径的精确上界，该上界与已知数学结果一致但尚待严格证明。

Conclusion: SharpDC在有限的规范群数量和大N条件下可被证明，但在某些大中心荷极限（如Class S理论中M→∞）下可能失效，这暗示SharpDC的普适性需要更细致的条件限定。RDC则可通过模空间几何获得更精确的数学刻画。该工作揭示了Distance Conjecture的精细版本在AdS背景下成立的边界条件。

Abstract: We test the Sharpened Distance Conjecture (SharpDC) for CFTs, as well as the Refined Distance Conjecture (RDC), in AdS$_5$ gravitational spacetimes, using the CFT dual. The former provides a lower bound on the exponential mass decay rate of the tower of states becoming light at infinite distance in the conformal manifold, while the latter bounds the bulk field range that can be traversed before the exponential behaviour of the tower kicks in. We prove the SharpDC for any large $N$ Lagrangian supersymmetric gauge theory with a finite number $M$ of gauge factors and vanishing one-loop $β$-function. We also investigate whether it universally holds in the limits of large central charge, finding that it does not: We exhibit concrete examples in Class S theories where the conjecture is violated in the $M\rightarrow\infty$ limit with $N$ fixed, where the size of the bulk internal geometry is much larger than the AdS curvature scale. We also test the RDC in the moduli space of Class S theories without punctures in the large $N$ limit, showing that it amounts to a precise upper bound on the Weil-Petersson diameter of the moduli space of Riemann surfaces of genus $g$. This bound is consistent with all the mathematical literature up to date, but so far remains unproven.

</details>


### [108] [The Lagrangian Amplituhedron](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28561)
*Syl Tom Krovi,Ran J. Tessler*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了amplituhedron的拉格朗日类比（Lagrangian analog），研究其几何性质及BCFW分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在散射振幅研究中，amplituhedron作为一种几何对象成功编码了树级和圈级的振幅信息。作者希望构造其拉格朗日版本，以探索更丰富的几何结构与物理对应关系。

Method: 将标准amplituhedron推广到拉格朗日几何框架中，构造其拉格朗日子流形结构，并通过BCFW递归方法研究其分解性质。

Result: 成功建立了拉格朗日amplituhedron的几何框架，并给出了其BCFW分解的结构性描述。

Conclusion: 拉格朗日amplituhedron为散射振幅提供了新的几何视角，其BCFW分解揭示了更深层的几何与物理联系。

Abstract: The article constructs the Lagrangian analog of the amplituhedron, studies its geometric properties and BCFW decompositions.

</details>
