### [109] [Quantum speed limit time of a uniformly accelerated Unruh-DeWitt detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27478)
*Debasish Ghosh,Bibhas Ranjan Majhi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在开放量子系统框架下，利用马尔可夫过程研究了均匀加速的Unruh-DeWitt探测器与无质量实标量场相互作用时的量子速度极限时间（QSLT）。研究覆盖三种场景：无边界有限温度闵可夫斯基时空、单边界（探测器平行加速）、以及双边界（探测器在边界间平行运动）。发现QSLT在特定加速度a附近出现显著特征变化，且双边界场景下临界加速度可低至约10^5 m/s²，这些特征可用于诊断Unruh效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Unruh效应作为量子场论与相对论交汇处的重要预言，其直接实验验证极为困难。作者希望通过研究QSLT这一量子信息论量在不同边界条件和温度下的演化特征，找到一种可行的Unruh效应诊断工具，为后续实验设计提供理论依据。

Method: 作者采用开放量子系统形式主义，在马尔可夫过程近似下描述均匀加速Unruh-DeWitt探测器与无质量实标量场的相互作用。分别考虑三种物理场景：(i)无边界但场处于有限温度；(ii)存在单边界且探测器平行于边界加速；(iii)存在双边界且探测器在边界间平行运动。通过求解并分析各场景下探测器的QSLT，考察其随加速度a的变化行为。

Result: QSLT在特定加速度值附近表现出非常独特的行为。第一种场景中，QSLT在特定a附近发生显著变化，且该临界a随场温度升高而减小；第二、三种场景中，QSLT在超过临界a后开始波动，且随a增大波动愈发剧烈。双边界场景的振荡起始加速度远小于单边界情况。考虑具体边界间距和探测器频率时，双边界场景的临界加速度可低至约10^5 m/s²。

Conclusion: QSLT在不同边界条件和温度下的独特行为模式可作为诊断Unruh效应的有效工具。特别是双边界场景中较低的临界加速度值，使得在可行实验参数范围内探测Unruh效应成为可能，为理论预言与实验验证之间架起了桥梁。

Abstract: Within the open quantum system formalism, using the Markovian process, we investigate the {\it quantum speed limit time} (QSLT) of a uniformly accelerated Unruh-DeWitt detector, interacting with the massless real scaler field. Three specific scenarios are being studied -- the field is in Minkowski spacetime (i) without any reflecting boundary but at a finite temperature, (ii) presence of single boundary with the detector is accelerating parallel to the plane of it, and (iii) presence of two boundaries with the detector is moving between them along the parallel direction. Interestingly, the behavior of QSLT appears to be very distinctive around a particular value of $a$. For the first scenario QSLT shows very noticeable change around a specific $a$ and the required value of $a$ corresponding to this change decreases with the increase of field temperature. In the other two scenarios, QSLT starts to fluctuate after a critical value of $a$. If we further increase $a$, more vigorous fluctuation emerges. The value of $a$ required for the starting of the oscillation is much less for the double boundary case compared to the single boundary. Taking into account specific values of the distance between the boundaries and the detector's frequency, we estimate that the critical value of $a$ can be as low as $\sim 10^5 \text{m}/\text{s}^2$ for the double boundary situation. We argue that such a distinctive feature in QSLT can be a fruitful tool to diagnose the Unruh effect.

</details>


### [110] [Creation of an inflationary epoch from an Emergent Universe through quantum tunneling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27597)
*Eduardo I. Guendelman,Ramon Herrera,Pedro Labraña*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在双标量场理论（Two Measures Theories, TMT）框架下，早期宇宙由爱因斯坦静态宇宙（Emergent Universe）描述，并通过量子隧穿从静态解过渡到膨胀相位。作者在WKB近似下计算了隧穿概率，发现由于有效势垒的对数贡献，隧穿概率呈现幂律而非指数依赖，且对参数而言接近1。宇宙随后进入超膨胀相并发展为慢滚暴涨。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统暴涨宇宙学依赖大爆炸奇点作为起点，而涌现宇宙（Emergent Universe）提供了一种避免大爆炸奇点的替代方案——宇宙在任意早时间都存在一个静态且经典稳定的爱因斯坦宇宙。作者希望在此框架下研究从静态宇宙到膨胀宇宙的量子隧穿转变机制及其概率特征。

Method: 在TMT中（具有自发破缺的标度不变性的理论，含跷跷板场dilaton），作者在爱因斯坦框架下研究对称破缺后的有效势。利用与dilaton相关的守恒量，将宇宙学演化重写为关于φ̇的有效势V(φ̇)，该势呈现发散势垒将静态解与后续膨胀演化分开。对相应的minisuperspace哈密顿量进行量子化，在WKB近似下计算隧穿概率，并固定平方根支从而赋予隧穿概率一致的半经典解释。

Result: 隧穿作用量明确定义且有限。势垒导致隧穿指数中出现对数贡献，使得隧穿概率呈幂律依赖而非通常的指数依赖；对考虑的参数值，隧穿概率接近1。宇宙以有限标度因子出现在超膨胀相中，进而发展为慢滚暴涨。值得注意的是，这种量子产生是宇宙从预存时空中隧穿而来，并非时空本身的量子创生。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，在TMT框架下，涌现宇宙的静态解通过量子隧穿可以以近单位概率过渡到暴涨相，避免了传统的大爆炸奇点和奇点量子创生问题。这一机制提供了一种无需时空量子创生即可产生暴涨宇宙的途径。

Abstract: We consider that the very early Universe was well described by an Emergent Universe, whose geometry was that of a static and classically stable Einstein Universe, which is possible in certain Two Measures Theories (TMT). These solutions do not have a big bang, but rather they extend to arbitrarily early times. The TMT we consider are theories with spontaneously broken scale invariance and contain a dilaton field, crucial for the implementation of the scale invariance, that after the symmetry breaking acquires a nontrivial effective potential, which we study in the Einstein frame. Using the conserved quantity associated with the dilaton, we recast the cosmological evolution in terms of an effective potential $V(\dotφ)$, which exhibits a divergent barrier separating the static solution from the subsequent expanding evolution. Quantizing the corresponding minisuperspace Hamiltonian, we evaluate the tunneling probability in the WKB approximation. The tunneling action is well defined and finite once the branch of the square root is fixed by requiring a consistent semiclassical interpretation of the tunneling probability. We find that the barrier gives rise to a logarithmic contribution to the tunneling exponent, yielding a power-law rather than the usual exponential dependence of the tunneling probability, which for the parameter values considered is close to unity. The Universe thus emerges with a finite scale factor into a superinflationary phase that develops into slow-roll inflation. This quantum creation of an inflationary Universe does not represent a quantum creation of spacetime, which exists before the tunneling.

</details>


### [111] [Thermodynamic Criticality in FLRW Cosmology with Non-extensive Loop Quantum Gravity Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27721)
*Miguel Cruz,Joel Saavedra,Juan Rodriguez*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有非广延圈量子引力修正熵的FLRW宇宙在表观视界上的热力学临界现象，发现仅在q>1分支出现有限物理临界点，系统属于标准平均场普适类，临界指数为(0,1/2,1,3)，Ruppeiner曲率在旋节线上发散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨非广延圈量子引力启发的熵在宇宙学表观视界热力学中的临界行为，验证该修正熵框架下是否存在有限物理临界点及其普适性。

Method: 采用完整的Kodama-Hayward温度和统一第一定律，在空间平坦FLRW宇宙中推导修正Friedmann动力学，构建视界状态方程，分析自旋节线、Gibbs自由能分支、相共存条件及临界指数，并计算归一化Ruppeiner曲率的临界标度行为。

Result: 临界点仅存在于q>1的非广延分支；Bekenstein-Hawking极限q→1下临界点被推向无穷远。自旋节线上热容量和等温压缩率发散，临界温度以下Gibbs自由能出现多分支，相共存满足温度、压强和Gibbs自由能相等。临界指数(0,1/2,1,3)表明属于平均场普适类。Ruppeiner曲率在自旋节线上发散，在临界等温线和临界等容线上分别呈R_N~-|v-v_c|^{-4}和R_N~-|t|^{-2}标度。临界膨胀标度为H_c^2=2(√5-2)|β|。

Conclusion: 该框架建立了表观视界有效热力学态空间自洽的临界结构，保留了平均场临界普适性，揭示熵形变达到单位量级时触发热力学临界点。

Abstract: We investigate thermodynamic criticality in a spatially flat Friedmann-Lemaître-Robertson-Walker universe with a non-extensive Loop Quantum Gravity inspired entropy on its apparent horizon. Using the full Kodama-Hayward temperature and the unified first law, we derive the modified Friedmann dynamics and construct the corresponding horizon equation of state. We see that a finite physical critical point only appears in the non-extensive branch $q>1$, while in the Bekenstein-Hawking limit $q\to1$, the critical point is continuously pushed to $v_c\to\infty$ and $T_c\to0$. Along the same spinodal curve, both the constant-pressure heat capacity and the isothermal compressibility become unbounded, and the extremum of this curve is located at the critical point. Below the critical temperature, the Gibbs free energy develops several thermodynamic branches, and the phase coexistence for different horizon states is verified by the equality of temperature, pressure, and Gibbs free energy. The critical exponents are $(α_{cr},β_{cr},γ_{cr},δ_{cr}) =(0,1/2,1,3)$, indicating that the system belongs to the standard mean-field universality class despite the non-algebraic form of the equation of state. The normalized Ruppeiner curvature diverges precisely on the spinodal curve and shows critical scaling $R_N\sim-|v-v_c|^{-4}$ on the critical isotherm, and $R_N\sim-|t|^{-2}$ on the critical isochore. Finally, the critical expansion scale is given by $H_c^2=2(\sqrt{5}-2)|β|$, which implies that the thermodynamic critical point occurs when the entropy deformation is of order unity. These results establish a self-consistent critical structure for the effective thermodynamic state space of the cosmological apparent horizon and retain the mean-field critical universality.

</details>


### [112] [Compact Core--Shell Equilibria in Gravitational Vlasov--Poisson Systems with Positive and Negative Mass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27900)
*Naoki Sato*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构造了具有正负质量分布的引力Vlasov-Poisson系统的球对称、规则、紧致能截断平衡态。在Bondi约定下，正负质量在中心重叠，形成"正质量壳+外部真空"的结构；在引力电荷约定下，两类质量空间分离，形成"负质量核+真空间隙+正质量壳+外部真空"的结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受Bondi负质量概念及其与带符号质能源的Einstein引力弱场极限相联系的启发，作者旨在研究负质量在恒星动力学中的分布规律，为在天体物理和宇宙学背景下研究负质量的分布提供动力学框架。

Method: 构造了正负质量分布的Vlasov-Poisson系统的球对称能截断平衡态。区分了两种质量约定：Bondi约定（惯性、被动引力和主动引力质量同变号，保持等效原理）和引力电荷约定（仅被动和主动引力质量变号，两类物质服从相反自由落体定律、异类质量相斥）。对截断指数n=-1/2给出了显式径向匹配解，对规则截断n=1/2则满足Lane-Emden型方程。

Result: Bondi约定下平衡态由包含两种质量物种的中心重叠核心、有限正质量壳层和外部真空组成；纯Bondi负质量气体无法形成紧致稳态，但可被正质量分布空间约束。引力电荷约定下平衡态空间分离为负质量核、真空间隙、正质量壳和外部真空。

Conclusion: 负质量的内部平衡结构定性取决于负质量的类型。该工作为在天体物理和宇宙学环境中研究负质量分布提供了动力学框架。

Abstract: We construct regular, spherically symmetric, compact energy-cutoff equilibria for gravitational Vlasov--Poisson systems sourced by positive and negative mass distributions in the context of stellar dynamics. Motivated by Bondi's notion of negative mass and its relation to the weak field limit of Einstein gravity with a signed mass--energy source, we show that the internal structure of the steady states depends qualitatively on the type of negative mass present. In the Bondi convention, the signs of inertial, passive gravitational, and active gravitational mass are reversed together, preserving the equivalence principle. The resulting equilibria consist of a central overlap core containing both mass species, surrounded by a finite positive mass shell and an exterior vacuum. Thus, although a pure Bondi negative mass gas cannot form a compact steady state, Bondi negative mass can be spatially confined by a suitable positive mass distribution. In the gravitational-charge convention, only the passive and active gravitational masses change sign, so that the two species obey opposite free-fall laws and unlike masses repel. The resulting equilibria are spatially segregated into a negative mass core, a vacuum gap, a positive mass shell, and an exterior vacuum. For cutoff exponent $n=-1/2$, the radial matching is explicit, while for the regular cutoff $n=1/2$ the matter regions satisfy Lane--Emden-type equations. These results provide a kinetic framework for investigating the distribution of negative mass in astrophysical and cosmological settings.

</details>


### [113] [Dynamical friction on black holes in scalar field environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28029)
*Adith Praveen,Dawood Kothawala*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过数值模拟研究了黑洞在复杂标量场中运动时所受的阻力（拖曳力），分别考察了恒定速度和恒定加速度两种情况。研究发现恒定速度情形下阻力在晚期饱和且与速度的依赖关系不同于已有文献；恒定加速度情形下阻力先增大到最大值F⋆≈0.3 m a后急剧减小，且密度尾迹呈现间隔递减的复兴现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天体物理中的黑洞并非存在于真空中，而是在非真空环境中运动，通过动力摩擦和Bondi吸积等过程会受到作用力。现有文献主要研究恒定速度情形，对恒定加速度情形的理解不足，因此需要对黑洞在标量场中的受力进行更全面的数值探索。

Method: 采用数值模拟方法研究黑洞穿过质量为m的复标量场的受力问题。恒定加速度情形用C-metric建模，恒定速度情形用Painlevé-Gullstrand度量并附加一个散度和涡度为零的速度场来模拟。分别对两种情形进行数值模拟并分析阻力随时间的演化。

Result: 恒定速度情形下，阻力在晚期饱和，其饱和值与速度的依赖关系与已有文献中已知关系不同。恒定加速度情形下，阻力增大到最大值F⋆≈0.3 m a后急剧减小；密度尾迹中出现时间间隔递减的复兴现象（revivals）。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了黑洞在标量场中运动时拖曳力的多个新颖且有趣的特性，特别是加速运动中阻力的非单调演化行为以及密度尾迹的复兴结构，这些发现对理解非真空环境中黑洞的动力学具有重要意义。

Abstract: Astrophysical black holes exist within non-vacuum environments, and their motion through these environments generically result in a force on these black holes through processes such as dynamical friction and Bondi accretion. We explore these forces numerically for black holes moving through a complex scalar field of mass $m$ with constant acceleration $a$, while also revisiting the constant velocity case considered in the existing literature. The former is modeled by the C-metric, while we use Painlevé-Gullstrand metric with an additional divergence and vorticity free velocity field to mimic the constant velocity motion. Our simulations reveal several novel and interesting aspects of the drag force in both cases. For the constant velocity case, the force saturates at late times, with a value that depends on velocity in a manner distinct from known dependence. For the constant acceleration case, the force increases to a maximum value $F_\star$ and then reduces drastically, with $F_\star \approx 0.3~ m a$. Moreover, the density wake in this case shows revivals separated by decreasing time intervals.

</details>


### [114] [Devoured by a Hairy Gargantua: Probing Massive Scalar Charges with Non-minimal Curvature Coupling with Extreme-Mass-Ratio Inspirals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28037)
*Leif Lui,Adrian Ka-Wai Chung,Alejandro Torres-Orjuela*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了非最小耦合到曲率的超轻标量场如何赋予旋转黑洞稳态毛发并改变附近轨道。通过使用近期构建的带毛黑洞时空，作者模拟了极端质量比并合（EMRI）的过渡到坠入过程。结果显示，在标量质量μM=0.01和0.1、无量纲曲率耦合ζ=10^-3和10^-4时，相对于广义相对论，在约10^3个轨道上产生了约10弧度的引力波去相位。在μM=0.2处的多模Fisher预测表明，对于信噪比约80的LISA源，ζ和μ的测量精度分别可达约0.2%和0.6%–1.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索超轻标量场非最小耦合曲率对黑洞周围引力波信号的影响，特别是在极端质量比旋进（EMRI）系统中，评估这种新物理效应能否被LISA等引力波探测器探测到并加以约束。

Method: 利用近期构造的带毛黑洞时空解，建立极端质量比旋进系统从绝热演化到最终坠入的过渡模型；采用多模Fisher矩阵预报方法，在μM=0.2处（该参数下相邻毛发解允许数值计算μ的导数）评估标量质量μ和曲率耦合ζ的可测量精度。

Result: 对于μM=0.01和0.1，以及ζ=10^-3和10^-4的参数组合，在约10^3个轨道上产生了约10弧度的引力波去相位（相对广义相对论）。Fisher预测显示，在信噪比约为80的LISA观测条件下，ζ和μ的测量精度分别可达约0.2%和0.6%–1.1%。

Conclusion: 非最小耦合的超轻标量场会在EMRI引力波信号中产生显著的相位偏差，且这一偏差足够大以致LISA可以在高信噪比下对这些参数进行高精度测量，从而为检验这种修改引力理论提供了可行的观测途径。

Abstract: Ultralight scalar fields nonminimally coupled to curvature can endow rotating black holes with stationary hair and alter nearby orbits. Using recently constructed hairy black-hole spacetimes, we model the transition to plunge of an extreme-mass-ratio inspiral. For scalar masses $μM=0.01$ and $0.1$ and dimensionless curvature coupling $ζ=10^{-3},10^{-4}$, we find $\mathcal{O}(10)$ rad of gravitational-wave dephasing relative to general relativity over $\mathcal{O}(10^3)$ orbits. A multimode Fisher forecast at $μM=0.2$, where neighbouring hair solutions permit a numerical $μ$-derivative, suggests that $ζ$ and $μ$ could be measured to precisions of $\simeq0.2\%$ and $0.6$--$1.1\%$, respectively, for a source with signal-to-noise ratio of about 80 in LISA.

</details>


### [115] [Photon Spheres and Shadows of Covariant Loop Quantum Black Holes in the general $μ$-scheme](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28186)
*Yu Han,Meng Liu,Yongzhuang Li*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了两类协变量子修正黑洞度规（BH-I和BH-II）的阴影与光子球性质，这些度规基于一般μ方案，由幂律指数s和振幅ξ参数化。研究发现BH-I在所有参数下均存在光子球并在s=1时恢复标准结果；BH-II的光子球在ξ超过临界值时消失。作者解析证明了光子球始终不稳定，推导了Lyapunov指数的闭式表达式，并通过EHT观测对(ξ,s)参数空间施加了约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子引力修正的协变量子修正黑洞度规中，之前文献通常将指数s固定为1（即μ̄方案），掩盖了更丰富的物理现象。作者希望将研究推广到一般s值（包括非整数），以揭示完整的参数空间行为，并探讨观测可行性。

Method: 作者将s从固定值1推广到一般幂律指数，对两类度规（BH-I和BH-II）系统地分析了光子球半径、阴影半径和Lyapunov指数。通过解析推导闭式表达式、小参数展开分析，并结合EHT观测数据进行参数约束和简并性分析。

Result: BH-I在s=1时恢复r_ph=3M的标准结果；BH-II存在临界曲线，ξ超过临界值后光子球消失。两类度规的光子球在所有物理参数空间内均不稳定。EHT约束表明BH-II的参数空间约束比BH-I紧2-4倍。单一阴影测量存在(ξ,s)参数简并，需联合Lyapunov指数测量才能打破简并。

Conclusion: 一般s参数化揭示了μ₀-方案和μ̄-方案之间更丰富的现象学行为。观测上，同时测量阴影和Lyapunov指数可以有效地约束量子修正参数空间，BH-II比BH-I更易受到观测限制。

Abstract: We investigate black hole shadows and photon sphere properties for two families of covariant quantum-corrected black-hole metrics (hereafter called BH-I and BH-II) formulated within a general $μ$-scheme, parameterised by a power-law exponent $s$ and an amplitude $ξ$. The extension to general (including non-integer) $s$ is a phenomenological interpolation between the $μ_0$-scheme ($s=0$) and $\barμ$-scheme ($s=1$) and reveals a rich phenomenology masked when $s$ is usually fixed to 1 in previous literature. For BH-I, the photon sphere exists for all parameter values and exhibits an exact cancellation at $s=1$ where the coordinate location $r_{\rm ph}=3M$ is restored for any $ξ$. For BH-II, both the horizon and the photon sphere exhibit critical curves in the $(ξ,s)$ parameter plane; the photon sphere disappears when $ξ$ exceeds a closed-form critical value $ξ_c^{\rm PS}(s)$. We prove analytically that the photon sphere is always unstable ($λ_{\rm ph}>0$) throughout the physical parameter space of both metrics, and derive closed-form expressions for the Lyapunov exponent. Systematic parameter scans reveal that for fixed $ξ$ the shadow radius decreases monotonically with $s$ for BH-II and non-monotonically for BH-I. We derive small-parameter analytic expansions for the photon-sphere radius, shadow radius, and Lyapunov exponent, and demonstrate that a single shadow measurement suffers an observational degeneracy in the two-dimensional $(ξ,s)$ space; the degeneracy can be broken by a simultaneous measurement of the Lyapunov exponent. Using Event Horizon Telescope (EHT) measurements, we derive constraints on the $(ξ,s)$ parameter space and find that BH-II is constrained roughly $2$--$4$ times more tightly than BH-I.

</details>


### [116] [Global structure of Kerr-Newman-BR spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28265)
*Hryhorii Ovcharenko*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Kerr-Newman-BR时空及其各种子情况（如Schwarzschild-BR时空）的整体结构，通过倒数坐标y=1/r证明了整个Kerr-Newman-BR时空族可以连续延拓穿过r→∞表面。然而，通过r→∞后的共形结构强烈依赖于时空类型（是否含旋转）以及所固定的极方向（一般极角θ、赤道面或两极附近）。最后讨论了这种连续延拓的普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究发现r→∞并非正确的共形无穷远，需要重新审视Kerr-Newman-BR时空的整体共形结构，理解其通过无穷远后的行为。

Method: 采用倒数坐标y=1/r对Kerr-Newman-BR时空族进行坐标变换，分析通过r→∞表面时的几何连续性，并分别考虑有旋转与无旋转情形，以及不同极方向（一般极角、赤道面、极轴附近）的行为。

Result: 证明了整个Kerr-Newman-BR时空族可以通过r→∞连续延拓；但延拓后的共形结构依赖于旋转存在与否以及所选的极方向。

Conclusion: 这种连续延拓具有某种普适性，但通过无穷远后的共形结构随时空参数和观测方向的不同而呈现丰富差异。

Abstract: We conduct an investigation of the global structure of Kerr-Newman-BR spacetime and its various subcases such as Schwarzschild-BR spacetime. In this work, we extend the result of our previous studies, where we found that $r\to \infty$ is not the proper conformal infinity. By using the reciprocal coordinate $y=1/r$, we show that the whole family of Kerr-Newman-BR spacetimes can be continuously extended through the surface $r\to \infty$. However, the conformal structure after passing $r\to\infty$ strongly depends on the type of spacetime one is considering (either rotation is present, or not) and on the polar direction one chooses to be fixed (namely, whether we consider generic polar angle $θ$, or we focus on the behaviour near the equatorial plane $θ=π/2$, or near the poles $θ=0,π$). Finally, we discuss the universality of such a continuous extension.

</details>

### [117] [Modular Functors with Singularities from Vertex Operator Algebras Beyond Rigidity and Finiteness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28579)
*Lukas Müller,Lukas Woike*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文针对顶点算子代数V及其合适模类别，提出了一种构造共形块空间的方法，将其组织为带奇点的开闭模函子。该方法基于全纯因子分解原理，通过模扩展策略（Costello引入，作者先前工作发展），为每个具有边界组件的曲面构造映射类群的表示。该构造在生成Dehn扭转上可显式描述，与代数几何或拓扑技术的构造方法独立但可比。在V的模类别是（不必半单的）模范畴A的特殊情形下，得到的空间与A的弦网空间及Drinfeld中心Z(A)的模函子等价。该构造的优势在于超越理性、刚性、自反性及有限性，并满足切除性质，在C2余有限情形下有限维，产生推广Brochier-Jordan的曲面辫群表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望直接从全纯因子分解原理出发，为顶点算子代数的模类别构造共形块空间，避免依赖代数几何或手术等技术，从而超越传统模函子构造所需的理性、半单性等限制，提供更广泛适用的模函子框架。

Method: 采用Costello的模扩展策略（modular extension），构建每个带边界曲面Σ的表示Ω_V(Σ;-)（模映射类群的表示），在生成Dehn扭转上显式描述。开发了带奇点（singularities）的开闭模函子概念，允许标记区间或边界圆上的边界标签，从而将模扩展推广到更一般的构形。

Result: 1）构建了Ω_V，满足开闭模函子的所有公理，且在生成Dehn扭转上可显式给出表示。2）当模类别为模范畴A时，Ω_V等价于A的弦网空间及Drinfeld中心Z(A)的模函子。3）证明Ω_V满足切除性，在C2余有限情形有限维。4）产生推广Brochier-Jordan的曲面辫群表示。5）对三重点代数W_{2,3}（非精确融合积）验证了Gaberdiel-Runkel-Wood的边界条件产生关联函数。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个独立于传统代数几何/拓扑方法的共形块构造途径，其适用性超越理性、刚性、自反性及有限性限制，且通过W_{2,3}的例子证明了带奇点模函子框架在非半单情形下的有效性。

Abstract: For a vertex operator algebra $V$ and a suitable category of its modules, we propose a construction for spaces of conformal blocks organized into an open-closed modular functor with singularities. This is inspired by the idea of implementing directly from the start the principle of holomorphic factorization. More precisely, using the strategy of modular extension introduced by Costello and developed further in our previous work, we build for each surface $Σ$ with at least one boundary component per path component and specified boundary labels attached to marked intervals or boundary circles a representation $Ω_V(Σ;-)$ of the mapping class group of $Σ$. The construction can be described explicitly on generating Dehn twists. This approach is a priori independent from other constructions based on algebraic geometry or topological techniques involving e.g. surgery, but we include an overview over the available comparisons. In the special case in which the module category of $V$ is a not necessarily semisimple modular category $\mathcal{A}$, the spaces $Ω_V(Σ)$ are equivalent to the string-net spaces for $\mathcal{A}$ and hence to the modular functor for the Drinfeld center $Z(\mathcal{A})\simeq \bar{\mathcal{A}}\boxtimes\mathcal{A}$. However, the construction of $Ω_V$ in this paper has the advantage of being available beyond rationality, rigidity, self-contragredience and finiteness. Moreover, we prove that $Ω_V$ satisfies excision, is finite-dimensional in the $C_2$-cofinite case and produces representations of surface braid groups generalizing the ones of Brochier-Jordan. We prove for the triplet $\mathcal{W}_{2,3}$ with non-exact fusion product that the boundary conditions introduced by Gaberdiel-Runkel-Wood produce correlation functions, provided that one uses the notion of a modular functor with singularities that we develop.

</details>
